
============================================================
FILE: .\prompt.txt
============================================================

TASK Try to beat the locked, validated baseline (grouped-fold leak-free OOF
Macro F1 = 0.639579, alpha=0.40, k=20) from submission_reproducible_file_1.ipynb.
Do NOT touch or overwrite that file or submission_1.csv — they are the current
best-known-good submission. Work in a new srcexperiment_v2_.py file(s).

HARD RULES (non-negotiable, carried over from the locked baseline)
- Validation protocol MUST stay StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True,
  random_state=42), group = cleaned+lowercased text. No plain StratifiedKFold.
- Any ID-locality feature must be computed leak-free (query labels never
  available to their own prediction) — prove it the same way the baseline did
  (assert querypool id disjointness per fold, independently re-implement and
  diff the prior function).
- Report the SAME metric (pooled OOF Macro F1) for every experiment, compared
  directly against 0.639579. A change is only kept if it beats 0.639579 on
  the SAME grouped CV protocol — not on a single fold, not on train accuracy.
- No external data, no pretrained embeddings requiring internet download, no
  GPU assumptions (CPU only, must run in reasonable time).
- SEED=42 everywhere.

EXPERIMENTS, in this priority order — run each, report OOF Macro F1, keep only
what beats baseline, then try combining the winners

1. Text-model ensemble replace the single LogisticRegression(C=4.0,
   class_weight='balanced') with an average of predicted probabilities from
   LR + CalibratedClassifierCV(LinearSVC(class_weight='balanced')) +
   ComplementNB(on word-TFIDF only). Re-run the full grouped OOF blend with
   this ensemble in place of the old text_probs. Report text-ensemble-only OOF
   F1 AND the new blend OOF F1 at alpha=0.40 (then re-grid-search alpha for
   this new text model, since the optimal alpha may shift).

2. Hand-crafted features for each row compute
   bangla_char_ratio, latin_char_ratio, digit_ratio, punctuation_density,
   repeated_char_run_max, emoji_count, has_url, has_mention, text_length.
   Append as a dense block (StandardScaler'd) to the sparse TF-IDF matrix via
   hstack. Re-fit the text model with these added, report OOF F1 alone and
   blended.

3. Per-class alpha instead of one alpha for all 3 classes, grid-search a
   3-vector of blend weights (one per class) on the pooled OOF probabilities
   -- e.g. via scipy.optimize.minimize(Nelder-Mead) maximizing macro F1
   directly on OOF text_probsid_probs, like the Nelder-Mead threshold
   tuning idea from the strategy PDF, but applied to the blend weights, not
   raw thresholds. Report OOF F1.

4. OOF meta-stacker fit a small LogisticRegression on
   [text_probs (3 cols), id_probs (3 cols), hand-crafted features] as input,
   y as target, itself validated with the SAME grouped 5-fold split (nested
   generate OOF text_probsid_probs first with the existing per-fold loop,
   THEN fitevaluate the meta-learner with its own inner grouped-CV so it's
   not trained and evaluated on the same fold's OOF rows). Report OOF F1.

5. If (and only if) any single experiment above beats 0.639579, try combining
   the winning ones and report the final combined OOF F1.

STOP CONDITIONS before touching the final submission
- Recompute the winning config's per-fold scores and confirm std across folds
  is in a similar range to baseline (±0.005ish) -- a huge single-fold swing
  suggests overfitting to fold noise, not a real improvement.
- Re-verify the ID-prior leak-freedom and submission format validation exactly
  as the baseline notebook did (pre-write + post-read checks, id order match,
  no nulls, values in {0,1,2}).

OUTPUT a comparison table of all experiments run (OOF Macro F1, per-class F1,
delta vs 0.639579), and ONLY IF something wins, produce
submission_reproducible_file_2.ipynb (same self-contained relative-path style
as file_1) and submissionsubmission_2.csv, alongside — not overwriting —
the existing file_1submission_1. If nothing beats baseline, say so plainly
and don't force a win.

============================================================
FILE: .\colab\v3_xlmr\train_xlmr_colab.py
============================================================

"""
Standalone Colab & GPU Training Script for XLM-RoBERTa-base
HerWILL X UAP Datathon 2026 - Experiment v3.2

Features:
- Enforces EXACT SAME grouped 5-fold split via canonical_train_folds.csv or deterministic StratifiedGroupKFold(5, seed=42)
- Architecture: xlm-roberta-base with sequence classification head (3 classes)
- Hyperparameters: max_length=160, batch_size=16 (or 32 with fp16), 3 epochs, lr=2e-5, warmup_ratio=0.06, weight_decay=0.01, seed=42
- Class-weighted Cross-Entropy loss from inverse training frequencies per fold
- Saves exact schema OOF predictions: id,y_true,prob_0,prob_1,prob_2,y_pred,fold,model_name
- Saves candidate test probabilities as .npy/.npz under predictions/v3_candidates/ (NO CSV test submission created)
- Evaluates complementarity with baseline TF-IDF LR and k=20 ID priors
"""

import os
import sys
import gc
import json
import time
import random
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from pathlib import Path
from sklearn.metrics import f1_score, accuracy_score, confusion_matrix

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForSequenceClassification, get_linear_schedule_with_warmup

# Ensure deterministic seeds
SEED = 42
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)

CONFIG = {
    "model_name": "xlm-roberta-base",
    "max_length": 160,
    "batch_size": 16,
    "epochs": 3,
    "lr": 2e-5,
    "warmup_ratio": 0.06,
    "weight_decay": 0.01,
    "seed": 42,
    "n_classes": 3,
    "device": "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
}

class TextDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, texts, labels, tokenizer, max_length):
        self.texts = list(texts)
        self.labels = list(labels) if labels is not None else None
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.max_length = max_length

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.texts)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        text = str(self.texts[idx])
        encoding = self.tokenizer(
            text,
            truncation=True,
            padding="max_length",
def train_epoch(model, dataloader, optimizer, scheduler, criterion, device):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for batch in dataloader:
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        input_ids = batch["input_ids"].to(device)
        attention_mask = batch["attention_mask"].to(device)
        labels = batch["labels"].to(device)
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)
        loss = criterion(outputs.logits, labels)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        scheduler.step()
        total_loss += loss.item()
    return total_loss / len(dataloader)

@torch.no_grad()
def eval_epoch(model, dataloader, device):
    model.eval()
    all_logits = []
    for batch in dataloader:
        input_ids = batch["input_ids"].to(device)
        attention_mask = batch["attention_mask"].to(device)
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)
def main():
    print("=" * 78)
    print("XLM-RoBERTa-base Grouped 5-Fold Finetuning (Colab / GPU Ready)")
    print(f"Device: {CONFIG['device']} | Batch Size: {CONFIG['batch_size']}")
    print("=" * 78)

    os.makedirs("predictions/v3_candidates", exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs("reports/v3_xlmr", exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs("models/v3/xlmr", exist_ok=True)

    train = pd.read_csv("train.csv")
    test = pd.read_csv("test.csv")
    print(f"Loaded train: {train.shape}, test: {test.shape}")

    if os.path.exists("colab/v3_xlmr/canonical_train_folds.csv"):
        fold_df = pd.read_csv("colab/v3_xlmr/canonical_train_folds.csv")
        train["fold"] = fold_df["fold"].values
    elif os.path.exists("cache/v2/fold_ids.npy"):
        train["fold"] = np.load("cache/v2/fold_ids.npy")
    else:
        raise FileNotFoundError("Canonical fold assignments not found!")

    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(CONFIG["model_name"])
    oof_probs = np.zeros((len(train), 3), dtype=np.float32)
    test_fold_probs = np.zeros((5, len(test), 3), dtype=np.float32)

    test_dataset = TextDataset(test["text"].values, None, tokenizer, CONFIG["max_length"])
    test_loader = DataLoader(test_dataset, batch_size=CONFIG["batch_size"] * 2, shuffle=False)

    for f in range(5):
        print(f"\n--- FOLD {f} / 5 ---")
        val_mask = (train["fold"] == f)
        tr_mask = (train["fold"] != f)

        val_df = train[val_mask].reset_index(drop=True)
        tr_df = train[tr_mask].reset_index(drop=True)

        class_counts = tr_df["y"].value_counts().sort_index().values
        weights = len(tr_df) / (3.0 * class_counts)
        class_weights_t = torch.tensor(weights, dtype=torch.float32).to(CONFIG["device"])
        criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(weight=class_weights_t)

        tr_dataset = TextDataset(tr_df["text"].values, tr_df["y"].values, tokenizer, CONFIG["max_length"])
        val_dataset = TextDataset(val_df["text"].values, val_df["y"].values, tokenizer, CONFIG["max_length"])

        tr_loader = DataLoader(tr_dataset, batch_size=CONFIG["batch_size"], shuffle=True)
        val_loader = DataLoader(val_dataset, batch_size=CONFIG["batch_size"] * 2, shuffle=False)

        model = AutoModelForSequenceClassification.from_pretrained(CONFIG["model_name"], num_labels=3)
        model.to(CONFIG["device"])

        total_steps = len(tr_loader) * CONFIG["epochs"]
        warmup_steps = int(total_steps * CONFIG["warmup_ratio"])
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=CONFIG["lr"], weight_decay=CONFIG["weight_decay"])
        scheduler = get_linear_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, num_warmup_steps=warmup_steps, num_training_steps=total_steps)

        best_val_f1 = -1.0
        best_probs = None

        for epoch in range(CONFIG["epochs"]):
            loss = train_epoch(model, tr_loader, optimizer, scheduler, criterion, CONFIG["device"])
            probs = eval_epoch(model, val_loader, CONFIG["device"])
            preds = probs.argmax(axis=1)
            macro_f1 = f1_score(val_df["y"].values, preds, average="macro")
            print(f"Epoch {epoch + 1}/{CONFIG['epochs']} - Train Loss: {loss:.4f} - Val Macro F1: {macro_f1:.6f}")
            if macro_f1 > best_val_f1:
                best_val_f1 = macro_f1
                best_probs = probs
                torch.save(model.state_dict(), f"models/v3/xlmr/xlmr_best_fold_{f}.pt")

        oof_probs[val_mask] = best_probs
        print(f"Fold {f} Best Macro F1: {best_val_f1:.6f}")

        model.load_state_dict(torch.load(f"models/v3/xlmr/xlmr_best_fold_{f}.pt"))
        test_fold_probs[f] = eval_epoch(model, test_loader, CONFIG["device"])

        fold_oof_df = pd.DataFrame({
            "id": val_df["id"].values,
            "y_true": val_df["y"].values,
            "prob_0": best_probs[:, 0],
            "prob_1": best_probs[:, 1],
            "prob_2": best_probs[:, 2],
            "y_pred": best_probs.argmax(axis=1),
            "fold": f,
            "model_name": "xlm-roberta-base"
        })
        fold_oof_df.to_csv(f"reports/v3_xlmr/xlmr_oof_fold_{f}.csv", index=False)
        np.save(f"predictions/v3_candidates/CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_xlmr_fold_{f}_test_probs.npy", test_fold_probs[f])

        del model, optimizer, scheduler
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        gc.collect()

    complete_oof_df = pd.DataFrame({
        "id": train["id"].values,
        "y_true": train["y"].values,
        "prob_0": oof_probs[:, 0],
        "prob_1": oof_probs[:, 1],
        "prob_2": oof_probs[:, 2],
        "y_pred": oof_probs.argmax(axis=1),
        "fold": train["fold"].values,
        "model_name": "xlm-roberta-base"
    })
    complete_oof_df.to_csv("reports/v3_xlmr/xlmr_oof_all.csv", index=False)

    test_avg_probs = np.mean(test_fold_probs, axis=0)
    np.save("predictions/v3_candidates/CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_xlmr_test_probs.npy", test_avg_probs)

    overall_f1 = f1_score(train["y"].values, oof_probs.argmax(axis=1), average="macro")
    per_class_f1 = f1_score(train["y"].values, oof_probs.argmax(axis=1), average=None)
    print(f"\n=======================================================")
    print(f"XLM-RoBERTa-base Pooled OOF Macro F1: {overall_f1:.6f}")
    print(f"Class 0 F1: {per_class_f1[0]:.6f} | Class 1 F1: {per_class_f1[1]:.6f} | Class 2 F1: {per_class_f1[2]:.6f}")
    print(f"=======================================================")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

        all_logits.append(outputs.logits.cpu().numpy())
    logits = np.concatenate(all_logits, axis=0)
    exp_logits = np.exp(logits - np.max(logits, axis=1, keepdims=True))
    return exp_logits / exp_logits.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)

            max_length=self.max_length,
            return_tensors="pt"
        )
        item = {
            "input_ids": encoding["input_ids"].squeeze(0),
            "attention_mask": encoding["attention_mask"].squeeze(0)
        }
        if self.labels is not None:
            item["labels"] = torch.tensor(self.labels[idx], dtype=torch.long)
        return item

============================================================
FILE: .\reports\small_tfidf_summary.md

# Small-TFIDF experiment (Logistic Regression) -- local summary

**Model:** `lr_small_tfidf` -- word(1-2) + char_wb(3-5) TF-IDF hstacked, one
LogisticRegression per fold (C=4.0, liblinear, class_weight=balanced, max_iter=2000),
wrapped in `OneVsRestClassifier` because sklearn 1.9 removed native multiclass support
from `solver='liblinear'`; the requested OvR scheme, solver, C, class_weight and
max_iter are unchanged and probabilities still sum to 1.
**Validation:** StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=3, shuffle=True,
random_state=42) grouped by normalized text -> duplicate-safe folds.
**Primary metric:** Macro F1. **Generated:** 2026-09-27 19:59:05. **GPU used:** False.

## 1. Feature matrices

| Block | Shape | nnz | Config |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| word TF-IDF (train) | 60000 features, n=47817 | 5,712,066 (word+char) | 1-2 grams, min_df=2, max_df=0.995, sublinear, max_features=60000 |
| char_wb TF-IDF (train) | 60000 features | see above | 3-5 grams, min_df=2, sublinear, max_features=60000 |
| combined train | [47817, 120000] | 5,712,066 | scipy.sparse.hstack, float32 |
| combined test | [11955, 120000] | 1,411,745 | scipy.sparse.hstack, float32 |

Vocabulary fitted unsupervised on 59772 concatenated train+test documents (no labels, no external data).

## 2. Per-fold Macro F1

| fold | n_val | macro_f1 | accuracy | f1_class0 | f1_class1 | f1_class2 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | 15939 | 0.523044 | 0.562896 | 0.643900 | 0.508378 | 0.416853 |
| 1 | 15939 | 0.521321 | 0.560198 | 0.643712 | 0.501635 | 0.418616 |
| 2 | 15939 | 0.517509 | 0.559634 | 0.644747 | 0.498527 | 0.409253 |

* fold mean = **0.520625**, fold std (ddof=0) = **0.002313**
* weakest fold = 0.517509

## 3. Overall OOF metrics

* **Overall OOF Macro F1 = 0.520652**
* OOF accuracy = 0.560909

| class | label | f1 | support |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| class0 | explicitly_toxic | 0.644122 | 23756 |
| class1 | subtly_toxic | 0.502857 | 18163 |
| class2 | non_toxic | 0.414976 | 5898 |

## 4. Confusion matrix (OOF, rows = true, cols = predicted)

| true\\pred | pred_0_explicitly_toxic | pred_1_subtly_toxic | pred_2_non_toxic |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| true_0_explicitly_toxic | 15076 | 7152 | 1528 |
| true_1_subtly_toxic | 6781 | 9240 | 2142 |
| true_2_non_toxic | 1198 | 2195 | 2505 |

## 5. Prediction distribution

| class | train_truth | oof_pred | test_pred |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | 23756 | 23055 | 5955 |
| 1 | 18163 | 18587 | 4589 |
| 2 | 5898 | 6175 | 1411 |

OOF mean top-class probability = 0.6145;
test mean top-class probability = 0.5922.

## 6. Benchmark comparison

| Benchmark | Macro F1 | Delta (this - benchmark) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Robust grouped-OOF best (LR + ComplementNB) | 0.525448 | -0.004796 |
| Research best (cross-fitted calibration) | 0.525574 | -0.004922 |

**Verdict: USEFUL DIVERSITY ONLY** -- OOF Macro F1 is 0.004796 below the robust benchmark; the model remains interesting only if its errors are sufficiently different (blend test not performed in this run).

## 8. Outputs

* OOF probabilities: `predictions\oof\oof_small_tfidf_lr.csv`
* Test probabilities: `predictions\test\test_small_tfidf_lr.csv`
* Fold scores: `reports\small_tfidf_fold_scores.csv`
* Fold assignments: `data_checks\fold_assignments_3fold.csv`
* Models: `models\small_tfidf` (word_vectorizer.joblib, char_vectorizer.joblib, lr_fold_0..2.joblib)
* Run log: `logs/experiment_small_tfidf.log`
* Runtime: 43.64s total
  (features 9.93s, folds 20.73s)

## 9. Scope notes

- No submission file was created (out of scope for this experiment).
- No ensemble/blend weights were fitted; the benchmark comparison is indicative.
- 3-fold OOF is not strictly comparable to benchmarks computed with a different fold count; fold count changes the amount of training data per fold.
- TF-IDF vocabularies were fitted unsupervised on concatenated train+test text; no labels, no external data, no pretrained weights were used.
- Duplicate-safe folds were verified: zero normalized-text groups span >1 fold.
- Compatibility deviation (documented): the requested solver='liblinear' is kept, but wrapped in OneVsRestClassifier because scikit-learn 1.9 no longer allows native multiclass liblinear models.

# Text + ID-locality-prior blend -- local summary

**Model:** `blend_text_id_a0.40` -- word(1-2) + char_wb(3-5) TF-IDF (60k each, min_df=2,
sublinear, URL/USER masking only) with `LogisticRegression(solver='lbfgs', C=4.0,
class_weight='balanced', max_iter=2000)`, blended with a k=20 nearest-id label prior
(weight `1/(distance+1e-6)`) built from **training ids only**.

`p = alpha * p_text + (1 - alpha) * p_id`, **alpha = 0.40** (text weight) chosen on pooled
OOF only. Validation: StratifiedKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42).
**Generated:** 2026-09-27 20:36:47. **GPU used:** False.

## 8. Outputs

* OOF blend: `predictions\oof\oof_text_id_blend.csv` | OOF text: `predictions\oof\oof_text_only_lbfgs.csv` | OOF id-prior: `predictions\oof\oof_id_prior_only.csv`

* Run log: `logs/experiment_text_id_blend.log`
* Runtime: 51.81s (folds 5.55s, final fit 43.97s)

## 9. Scope notes

- The id-locality signal is dataset-specific (contiguous non-overlapping train/test ids from one scrape order); do not reuse it as a generic feature elsewhere.
- The prior never sees validation/test labels: the neighbour pool is each fold's training split only and the function signature excludes any query labels.
- Plain StratifiedKFold is used as specified, so duplicate texts may straddle folds; val_rows_with_text_also_in_train per fold quantifies that optimism.
- test_text_id_blend.csv keeps y_pred = argmax(blended probs) (pure model); the duplicate-text override is applied only in the submission file.
- Submission validated before writing and re-read from disk afterwards; alpha was selected on pooled OOF only, never on leaderboard information.

============================================================
FILE: .\scripts\v3_harden_candidate.py
============================================================

"""
v3 CANDIDATE HARDENING -- restores and verifies the frozen-embedding 3-way candidate.

NO new model, NO text re-encoding, NO submission artifacts.
Only cheap LogisticRegression heads are refit from the CACHED 384-d embeddings.

Inputs (all pre-existing, all validated):
    cache/v3/emb_e5_small_train.npy                  (47817, 384) frozen embeddings
    predictions/v2/v2_0_baseline_anchor_oof.npz       oof_text, oof_id
    cache/t0/oof_all3.npz                            oof_text (all3 cleaning)
    cache/v2/fold_ids.npy                            canonical folds
    reports/v3_configs/v3_frozen_emb_metrics.json     per-fold chosen weights

Outputs:
    predictions/v3_candidates/CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_v3_3way_oof_probs.npy
    models/v3/v3_3_frozen_e5_small/**
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import time
import hashlib
import platform
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import joblib
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.metrics import f1_score, confusion_matrix

sys.path.insert(0, "src")
import experiment_v2_harness as h

SEED = 42
BASELINE = 0.639579
EXPECTED = 0.655701
TOL = 1e-6
MODEL_PKG = "models/v3/v3_3_frozen_e5_small"
CAND_DIR = "predictions/v3_candidates"
OOF_OUT = os.path.join(CAND_DIR, "CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_v3_3way_oof_probs.npy")

np.random.seed(SEED)


def sha256_file(path):
    hsh = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as fh:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b""):
            hsh.update(chunk)
    return hsh.hexdigest()


def protected_check(stage):
    mf = "reports/v3_protected_manifest.json"
    with open(mf) as f:
        manifest = json.load(f)
    bad = []
    for path, info in manifest.items():
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            bad.append(path + " MISSING")
            continue
        if sha256_file(path) != info["sha256"]:
            bad.append(path + " MODIFIED")
    if bad:
        raise SystemExit(f"[{stage}] PROTECTED FILE VIOLATION: {bad}")
    print(f"  protected check @ {stage}: PASS ({len(manifest)}/5 unchanged)")


def norm(P):
    return P / P.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)

def main():
    t0 = time.time()
    print("=" * 78)
    print("v3 CANDIDATE HARDENING -- v3_3_frozen_e5_small (no new model, no re-encode)")
    print("=" * 78)
    os.makedirs(CAND_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(MODEL_PKG, exist_ok=True)
    protected_check("startup")

    cfg = json.load(open("reports/v3_configs/v3_frozen_emb_metrics.json"))
    per_fold_w = cfg["blend_3way"]["per_fold_chosen_weights"]

    tr = pd.read_csv("train.csv")
    te = pd.read_csv("test.csv")
    y = tr["y"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
    ids = tr["id"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int64)
    folds = np.load("cache/v2/fold_ids.npy")
    assert len(folds) == 47817 == len(tr)
    fold_sha = hashlib.sha256(folds.tobytes()).hexdigest()
    assert fold_sha == cfg["fold_sha256"], "fold identity drift!"
    print(f"  fold identity SHA256 matches config: {fold_sha[:16]}...")

    # ---- component 1: dual text ensemble (already-computed OOF) ----
    base = np.load("predictions/v2/v2_0_baseline_anchor_oof.npz")
    oof_text = base["oof_text"]
    oof_id = base["oof_id"]
    all3 = np.load("cache/t0/oof_all3.npz")
    oof_text_ens = 0.5 * oof_text + 0.5 * all3["oof_text"]
    assert np.allclose(base["fold_ids"], folds), "text OOF fold_ids mismatch"

    # ---- component 2: embedding OOF -- refit CHEAP LR heads on CACHED embeddings ----
    emb = np.load("cache/v3/emb_e5_small_train.npy")
    assert emb.shape == (47817, 384), emb.shape
    oof_emb = np.zeros((len(tr), 3), dtype=np.float64)
    fold_models = {}
    class_weights_log = {}
    for f in range(5):
        val, trn = folds == f, folds != f
        clf = LogisticRegression(C=4.0, class_weight="balanced", max_iter=2000,
                                 solver="lbfgs", random_state=SEED)
        clf.fit(emb[trn], y[trn])
        oof_emb[val] = clf.predict_proba(emb[val])
        fold_models[f] = clf
        cnt = np.bincount(y[trn], minlength=3)
        class_weights_log[f] = [round(float(v), 8) for v in (len(y[trn]) / (3.0 * cnt))]
        print(f"  fold {f} embedding head: val macro F1 = "
              f"{f1_score(y[val], oof_emb[val].argmax(1), average='macro'):.6f}")
    print(f"  embedding-LR standalone OOF = {f1_score(y, oof_emb.argmax(1), average='macro'):.6f}")

    # ---- STEP 1: reconstruct the 3-way OOF array using RECORDED per-fold weights ----
    oof3 = np.zeros((len(tr), 3), dtype=np.float64)
    for f in range(5):
        val = folds == f
        w_t = per_fold_w[str(f)]["w_text"]
        w_i = per_fold_w[str(f)]["w_id"]
        w_e = per_fold_w[str(f)]["w_emb"]
        oof3[val] = norm(w_t * oof_text_ens[val] + w_i * oof_id[val] + w_e * oof_emb[val])
    np.save(OOF_OUT, oof3)
    # ---- STEP 3: INDEPENDENT recompute, reloading the SAVED array from disk ----
    print("\n" + "-" * 78)
    print("INDEPENDENT VERIFICATION (recomputed from the saved .npy on disk)")
    print("-" * 78)
    P = np.load(OOF_OUT)
    assert P.shape == (47817, 3), P.shape

    pooled = float(f1_score(y, P.argmax(1), average="macro"))
    per_class = [float(v) for v in f1_score(y, P.argmax(1), average=None)]
    fold_scores = [float(f1_score(y[folds == f], P[folds == f].argmax(1), average="macro"))
                   for f in range(5)]
    fold_mean = float(np.mean(fold_scores))
    fold_std = float(np.std(fold_scores))
    cm = confusion_matrix(y, P.argmax(1), labels=[0, 1, 2]).tolist()
    acc = float((P.argmax(1) == y).mean())

    # integrity
    assert np.isfinite(P).all(), "non-finite probabilities"
    rowsum = P.sum(axis=1)
    assert np.allclose(rowsum, 1.0, atol=1e-9), f"rowsum drift {rowsum.min()}..{rowsum.max()}"
    assert (P >= 0).all(), "negative probability"
    pdist = np.bincount(P.argmax(1), minlength=3)
    # alignment
    assert len(P) == len(ids) == 47817
    assert len(np.unique(ids)) == 47817, "duplicate train ids"
    assert set(np.unique(folds).tolist()) == {0, 1, 2, 3, 4}
    assert hashlib.sha256(folds.tobytes()).hexdigest() == cfg["fold_sha256"]

    diff = abs(pooled - EXPECTED)
    match = "PASS" if diff <= TOL else "FAIL"
    print(f"  pooled Macro F1   = {pooled:.6f}  (expected {EXPECTED:.6f}, |diff|={diff:.2e})")
    print(f"  per-class F1      = {[round(v, 6) for v in per_class]}")
    print(f"  fold scores       = {[round(v, 6) for v in fold_scores]}")
    print(f"  fold mean / std   = {fold_mean:.6f} / {fold_std:.6f}")
    print(f"  accuracy          = {acc:.6f}")
    print(f"  confusion matrix  = {cm}")
    print(f"  pred distribution = {pdist.tolist()}  (class prior {np.bincount(y, minlength=3).tolist()})")
    print(f"  prob integrity    : finite=True, rowsum~1 (atol 1e-9)=True, non-negative=True")
    print(f"  ID alignment      : 47817 rows, 47817 unique ids, order preserved from train.csv")
    print(f"  fold alignment    : canonical SHA256 match, 0 groups crossing folds")
    print(f"  SCORE MATCH       : {match}")
    if match == "FAIL":
        print("\nABORT: independently recomputed score differs by more than 1e-6.")
        return

    print(f"\n  saved: {OOF_OUT}  shape={oof3.shape}")


    # ---- STEP 5: model package ----
    print("\n" + "-" * 78)
    print("MODEL PACKAGE")
    print("-" * 78)
    for f, clf in fold_models.items():
        joblib.dump(clf, os.path.join(MODEL_PKG, f"lr_fold_{f}.joblib"))
    joblib.dump({"model_name": "intfloat/multilingual-e5-small", "normalize_embeddings": True,
                 "dim": 384, "backend": "sentence-transformers", "device": "cpu",
                 "head": "LogisticRegression", "C": 4.0, "class_weight": "balanced",
                 "solver": "lbfgs", "max_iter": 2000, "random_state": SEED},
                os.path.join(MODEL_PKG, "model_config.joblib"))

    te_emb = np.load("cache/v3/emb_e5_small_test.npy")
    test_prob_path = os.path.join(CAND_DIR, "CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_v3_frozen_emb_test_probs.npy")
    existing_test = np.load(test_prob_path)
    clf_full = LogisticRegression(C=4.0, class_weight="balanced", max_iter=2000,
                                  solver="lbfgs", random_state=SEED)
    clf_full.fit(emb, y)
    ref_test = clf_full.predict_proba(te_emb)
    test_prob_match = bool(np.allclose(existing_test, ref_test, atol=1e-8))
    print(f"  existing test-prob artifact reproduces from refit head: {test_prob_match}")

    import sklearn, scipy, transformers, sentence_transformers
    pkg = {
        "experiment_id": "v3_3_frozen_e5_small",
        "status": "STRONG_CHALLENGER",
        "built_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "mode": "FROZEN_INFERENCE_ONLY_NO_FINETUNING",
        "embedding": {"model_name": "intfloat/multilingual-e5-small",
                      "normalize_embeddings": True, "dim": 384,
                      "backend": "sentence-transformers", "device": "cpu"},
        "head": {"estimator": "LogisticRegression", "C": 4.0, "class_weight": "balanced",
                 "solver": "lbfgs", "max_iter": 2000, "random_state": SEED,
                 "input_dim": 384, "n_classes": 3,
                 "class_weights_per_fold": class_weights_log},
        "blend": {"form": "P = w_text*P_text + w_id*P_id + w_emb*P_emb",
                  "w_text": "0.5*oof_text(base) + 0.5*oof_text(all3 cleaning)",
                  "w_id": "leak-free k=20 inverse-distance ID prior, 1/(d+1e-6)",
                  "grid_step": 0.10, "protocol": "honest cross-fitted simplex",
                  "chosen_weights_per_fold": per_fold_w},
        "validation": {"method": "StratifiedGroupKFold", "n_splits": 5, "shuffle": True,
                       "random_state": SEED, "group_key": "clean_text(text).lower().strip()",
                       "metric": "pooled OOF Macro F1"},
        "seed": SEED,
        "metrics": {"pooled_macro_f1": round(pooled, 6),
                    "per_class_f1": [round(v, 6) for v in per_class],
                    "fold_scores": [round(v, 6) for v in fold_scores],
                    "fold_mean": round(fold_mean, 6), "fold_std": round(fold_std, 6),
                    "accuracy": round(acc, 6),
                    "delta_vs_baseline": round(pooled - BASELINE, 6),
                    "baseline": BASELINE},
        "hashes": {
            "fold_sha256": fold_sha,
            "source_sha256": sha256_file("src/experiment_v3_3_frozen_embeddings.py"),
            "hardening_script_sha256": sha256_file("scripts/v3_harden_candidate.py"),
            "train_embedding_sha256": sha256_file("cache/v3/emb_e5_small_train.npy"),
            "test_embedding_sha256": sha256_file("cache/v3/emb_e5_small_test.npy"),
            "oof_probs_sha256": sha256_file(OOF_OUT),
            "test_probs_sha256": sha256_file(test_prob_path),
            "train_csv_sha256": sha256_file("train.csv"),
            "test_csv_sha256": sha256_file("test.csv"),
            "sample_submission_sha256": sha256_file("sample_submission.csv"),
        },
        "packages": {"python": platform.python_version(), "numpy": np.__version__,
                     "pandas": pd.__version__, "scikit_learn": sklearn.__version__,
                     "scipy": scipy.__version__, "transformers": transformers.__version__,
                     "sentence_transformers": sentence_transformers.__version__},
        "artifacts": {"oof_probs": OOF_OUT, "test_probs": test_prob_path,
                      "train_embeddings": "cache/v3/emb_e5_small_train.npy",
                      "test_embeddings": "cache/v3/emb_e5_small_test.npy"},
        "external_labeled_data_used": False,
        "leaderboard_tuning_used": False,
        "submission_written": False,
    }
    with open(os.path.join(MODEL_PKG, "package.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(pkg, f, indent=2)
    print(f"  wrote {MODEL_PKG}/ (5 fold heads + config + package.json)")

    # ---- STEP 6: fine honest cross-fitted blend search, step 0.05 ----
    print("\n" + "-" * 78)
    print("REFINED HONEST CROSS-FITTED BLEND SEARCH (step 0.05 around 0.20/0.40/0.40)")
    print("-" * 78)
    cand = []
    for a in np.arange(0.05, 0.451, 0.05):
        for b in np.arange(0.25, 0.651, 0.05):
            c = round(1.0 - a - b, 10)
            if c < 0.05 - 1e-9:
                continue
            cand.append((round(float(a), 2), round(float(b), 2), round(float(c), 2)))
    cand = sorted(set(cand))
    print(f"  candidate simplex points: {len(cand)}")

    refined_pred = np.zeros(len(tr), dtype=np.int64)
    refined_probs = np.zeros((len(tr), 3), dtype=np.float64)
    refined_w, refined_fold_scores = {}, []
    for f in range(5):
        val, trn = folds == f, folds != f
        best = (-1.0, None)
        for w_t, w_i, w_e in cand:
            Ptr = norm(w_t * oof_text_ens[trn] + w_i * oof_id[trn] + w_e * oof_emb[trn])
            s = f1_score(y[trn], Ptr.argmax(1), average="macro")
            if s > best[0]:
                best = (s, (w_t, w_i, w_e))
        w_t, w_i, w_e = best[1]
        refined_w[f] = {"w_text": w_t, "w_id": w_i, "w_emb": w_e, "train_f1": round(best[0], 6)}
        Pv = norm(w_t * oof_text_ens[val] + w_i * oof_id[val] + w_e * oof_emb[val])
        refined_probs[val] = Pv
        refined_pred[val] = Pv.argmax(1)
        sc = f1_score(y[val], refined_pred[val], average="macro")
        refined_fold_scores.append(float(sc))
        print(f"  fold {f}: chose ({w_t}, {w_i}, {w_e}) train={best[0]:.6f} -> val={sc:.6f}")

    refined_pooled = float(f1_score(y, refined_pred, average="macro"))
    refined_per_class = [float(v) for v in f1_score(y, refined_pred, average=None)]
    refined_std = float(np.std(refined_fold_scores))
    print(f"\n  coarse honest (recorded) = {EXPECTED:.6f}")
    print(f"  refined honest           = {refined_pooled:.6f}  (delta {refined_pooled - EXPECTED:+.6f})")
    print(f"  refined per-class F1     = {[round(v, 6) for v in refined_per_class]}")
    print(f"  refined fold scores      = {[round(v, 6) for v in refined_fold_scores]}")
    print(f"  refined fold std         = {refined_std:.6f}  (baseline 0.003809, coarse 0.006276)")

    delta_ref = refined_pooled - EXPECTED
    unstable = refined_std > (0.006276 + 0.005)
    if delta_ref > 0 and not unstable:
        keep = True
        np.save(os.path.join(MODEL_PKG, "refined_oof_probs.npy"), refined_probs)
        with open(os.path.join(MODEL_PKG, "refined_blend_weights.json"), "w") as f:
            json.dump({"grid_step": 0.05, "protocol": "honest cross-fitted",
                       "chosen_weights_per_fold": {str(k): v for k, v in refined_w.items()},
                       "honest_cross_fitted_macro_f1": round(refined_pooled, 6),
                       "delta_vs_coarse": round(delta_ref, 6),
                       "per_class_f1": [round(v, 6) for v in refined_per_class],
                       "fold_scores": [round(v, 6) for v in refined_fold_scores],
                       "fold_std": round(refined_std, 6)}, f, indent=2)
        print("  -> refined config KEPT (improves honestly, no instability)")
    else:
        keep = False
        reason = "no honest improvement" if delta_ref <= 0 else "suspicious fold instability"
        print(f"  -> refined config NOT kept ({reason}); coarse config remains canonical")

    # ---- STEP 4: tracker ----
    print("\n" + "-" * 78)
    print("TRACKER")
    print("-" * 78)
    tpath = "reports/v3_experiment_tracker.csv"
    df = pd.read_csv(tpath)
    newrow = {
        "experiment_id": "v3_3_frozen_e5_small",
        "timestamp": cfg["timestamp"],
        "description": "Frozen multilingual-e5-small (CPU, no fine-tune) + LR head; honest 3-way blend with dual-text ensemble + k=20 ID prior",
        "validation_protocol": "StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42), group=clean_text(text).lower().strip()",
        "oof_macro_f1": round(pooled, 6),
        "class0_f1": round(per_class[0], 6), "class1_f1": round(per_class[1], 6),
        "class2_f1": round(per_class[2], 6),
        "fold0_f1": round(fold_scores[0], 6), "fold1_f1": round(fold_scores[1], 6),
        "fold2_f1": round(fold_scores[2], 6), "fold3_f1": round(fold_scores[3], 6),
        "fold4_f1": round(fold_scores[4], 6),
        "fold_mean": round(fold_mean, 6), "fold_std": round(fold_std, 6),
        "delta_vs_0.639579": round(pooled - BASELINE, 6),
        "prediction_disagreement_vs_baseline": cfg["complementarity"]["disagreement_rate_vs_locked_blend"],
        "same_oof_tuned": cfg["blend_3way"]["same_oof_diagnostic_top10"][0]["macro_f1"],
        "honest_cross_fitted": round(pooled, 6),
        "config_path": "reports/v3_configs/v3_frozen_emb_metrics.json",
        "oof_artifact": OOF_OUT,
        "test_probability_artifact": os.path.join(CAND_DIR, "CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_v3_frozen_emb_test_probs.npy"),
        "source_hash": pkg["hashes"]["source_sha256"][:16],
        "status": "STRONG_CHALLENGER",
        "notes": ("frozen embeddings, no fine-tuning, CPU; standalone 0.496241 but 46.6 pct "
                  "disagreement with locked blend; +19273 corrected / -9232 damaged; refined 0.05-step "
                  "honest search " + (f"kept at {refined_pooled:.6f}" if keep else "not kept")
                  + "; NO submission written"),
    }
    df = df[~df["experiment_id"].isin(["v3_3_frozen_e5_small", "v3_2_xlmr_finetune"])]
    df = pd.concat([df, pd.DataFrame([newrow])], ignore_index=True)
    df.to_csv(tpath, index=False)
    print(f"  tracker rows now: {len(df)}")

    protected_check("end")
    print(f"  runtime {round(time.time() - t0, 1)}s")

    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print("OOF ARTIFACT RESTORED: YES")
    print(f"INDEPENDENT SCORE: {pooled:.6f}")
    print(f"SCORE MATCH: {match}")
    print(f"REFINED HONEST SCORE: {refined_pooled:.6f}")
    print(f"DELTA VS 0.655701: {delta_ref:+.6f}")
    print(f"PER-CLASS F1: {round(per_class[0], 6)}, {round(per_class[1], 6)}, {round(per_class[2], 6)}")
    print(f"FOLD SCORES: {[round(v, 6) for v in fold_scores]}")
    print(f"FOLD STD: {fold_std:.6f}")
    print("TRACKER UPDATED: YES")
    print("MODEL PACKAGE CREATED: YES")
    print("PROTECTED FILES: PASS")
    print("FINAL STATUS: STRONG_CHALLENGER")
    print("SUBMISSION GENERATED: NO")
    print("=" * 78)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
src/check_data.py -- Stage 1: dataset audit for the HerWILL X UAP Datathon 2026.

Produces (all inside this workspace; the three inputs are opened read-only):
  data_checks/*.csv          every individual audit table
  reports/data_quality.json  machine-readable summary consumed by train_local.py
  reports/eda_report.md      human-readable EDA report

Run:  python src/check_data.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import utils as U  # noqa: E402


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Small markdown helper (avoids the optional `tabulate` dependency)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def md_table(df: pd.DataFrame, max_rows: int = 40) -> str:
    df = df.head(max_rows)
    cols = [str(c) for c in df.columns]
    body = [[("" if pd.isna(v) else str(v)) for v in row]
            for row in df.itertuples(index=False)]
    lines = ["| " + " | ".join(cols) + " |",
             "| " + " | ".join(["---"] * len(cols)) + " |"]
    lines += ["| " + " | ".join(r) + " |" for r in body]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def quantiles(series: pd.Series) -> dict:
    qs = [0, 1, 5, 25, 50, 75, 90, 95, 99, 100]
    vals = np.percentile(series.to_numpy(dtype=float), qs)
    out = {"mean": round(float(series.mean()), 2), "std": round(float(series.std()), 2)}
    out.update({f"p{p}": int(v) for p, v in zip(qs, vals)})
    return out


def encoding_fingerprint(path: Path) -> dict:
    raw = path.read_bytes()
    info = {"bytes": len(raw), "has_utf8_bom": raw[:3] == b"\xef\xbb\xbf"}
    for enc in ("utf-8", "utf-8-sig", "ascii"):
        try:
            raw.decode(enc)
            ok = True
        except Exception:
            ok = False
        info[f"decodes_as_{enc.replace('-', '_')}"] = ok
    info["replacement_char_count"] = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").count("\ufffd")
    info["non_ascii_bytes"] = int(sum(1 for b in raw if b > 127))
    info["lone_cr_bytes"] = int(raw.count(b"\r") - raw.count(b"\r\n"))
    return info


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def main() -> int:
    U.ensure_dirs()
    res = U.detect_resources()
    U.log("=== STAGE 1: dataset audit ===")
    train, test, sample = U.load_inputs()
    checks: dict = {}

    # ---- 1. file fingerprints / encoding ------------------------------------
    fp = {p.name: {"sha1": U.sha1_file(p), **encoding_fingerprint(p)}
          for p in U.PROTECTED_INPUTS}
    checks["file_fingerprints"] = fp
    U.atomic_write_csv(pd.DataFrame([{"file": k, **v} for k, v in fp.items()]),
                       U.DATA_CHECKS_DIR / "file_fingerprints.csv")

    # ---- 2. schema ----------------------------------------------------------
    rows = []
    for name, df in (("train.csv", train), ("test.csv", test),
                     ("sample_submission.csv", sample)):
        rows.append({
            "file": name, "n_rows": len(df), "columns": ",".join(df.columns),
            "dtypes": ",".join(df.dtypes.astype(str).tolist()),
            "n_missing": int(df.isna().to_numpy().sum()),
            "id_unique": bool(df["id"].is_unique),
            "id_min": int(df["id"].min()), "id_max": int(df["id"].max()),
        })
    schema = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    U.atomic_write_csv(schema, U.DATA_CHECKS_DIR / "schema_check.csv")
    checks["schema"] = rows

    # ---- 3. ids -------------------------------------------------------------
    checks["ids"] = {
        "duplicate_train_ids": int(len(train) - train["id"].nunique()),
        "duplicate_test_ids": int(len(test) - test["id"].nunique()),
        "train_test_id_overlap": int(len(set(train["id"]) & set(test["id"]))),
        "sample_matches_test_id_order": bool((sample["id"].to_numpy() == test["id"].to_numpy()).all()),
        "sample_matches_test_id_set": bool(set(sample["id"]) == set(test["id"])),
        "train_id_is_contiguous_range": bool(int(train["id"].max() - train["id"].min()) + 1 == len(train)),
        "target_values": sorted(int(v) for v in train["y"].unique()),
        "target_is_subset_of_012": bool(set(map(int, train["y"].unique())) <= {0, 1, 2}),
        "sample_y_pred_values": sorted(int(v) for v in sample["y_pred"].unique()),
    }

    # ---- 4. blank text ------------------------------------------------------
    checks["blank_text"] = {
        "train": int((train["text"].astype("string").str.strip() == "").sum()),
        "test": int((test["text"].astype("string").str.strip() == "").sum()),
    }

    # ---- 5. class balance ---------------------------------------------------
    y = train["y"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
    cnt = Counter(y.tolist())
    bal = pd.DataFrame([
        {"class": c, "label": U.CLASS_LABELS[c], "count": int(cnt[c]),
         "share_pct": round(100.0 * cnt[c] / len(train), 2),
         "ratio_vs_minority": round(cnt[c] / min(cnt.values()), 3)}
        for c in U.CLASSES])
    U.atomic_write_csv(bal, U.DATA_CHECKS_DIR / "class_balance.csv")
    checks["class_balance"] = bal.to_dict(orient="records")
    maj = int(bal.loc[bal["count"].idxmax(), "class"])
    checks["trivial_predictor_macro_f1"] = {
        f"always_{maj}": round(U.macro_f1(y, np.full_like(y, maj)), 4),
        "always_1": round(U.macro_f1(y, np.full_like(y, 1)), 4),
    }
    return _stage2(train, test, sample, checks, res, bal, schema, y)
