============================================================

"""
src/experiment_v2_4_meta_stacker.py
===================================

EXPERIMENT 4 (prompt priority 4)
---------------------------------
OOF meta-stacker: a small LogisticRegression on

    [ text_probs (3 cols), id_probs (3 cols), hand-crafted features (9 cols) ]

with y as target, itself validated with the SAME grouped 5-fold split
(StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42), group =
clean_text.lower().strip()). The OOF base probabilities come from the existing
per-fold loop, so the meta-learner is never trained and evaluated on the same
fold's rows.

Two variants are reported:

A. `spec`      -- exactly the prompt's protocol: base OOF matrices from the
                  single per-fold loop, meta-learner cross-fitted with its own
                  grouped 5-fold CV.
B. `strict`    -- an extra, more conservative check in which the meta-TRAIN rows'
                  base features are regenerated by base models that never saw
                  the meta-evaluation fold either (a genuinely double-nested
                  loop, 20 extra text fits). A row's own label still never
                  reaches its own prediction in either variant; variant B also
                  removes the milder "base model saw the eval fold" optimism.

Ablations reported for free: probs-only (no hand features), hand-features-only,
single feature group, and the raw 2-source blend for reference.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

import experiment_v2_harness as H
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedGroupKFold
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

META_KW = dict(C=1.0, max_iter=2000, class_weight="balanced", random_state=H.SEED)
META_FEATURES = ([f"text_p{c}" for c in H.CLASSES] + [f"id_p{c}" for c in H.CLASSES]
                 + H.HAND_FEATURE_NAMES)


def meta_folds(y, clean_texts):
    """The SAME grouped 5-fold split, recomputed through its own call and
    asserted to equal the outer fold ids."""
    keys = pd.Index([H.group_key(t) for t in clean_texts], dtype="object")
    groups = pd.factorize(keys, sort=False)[0]
    sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=H.N_SPLITS, shuffle=True, random_state=H.SEED)
    fold_ids = np.full(len(y), -1, dtype=int)
    for k, (_tr, va) in enumerate(sgkf.split(np.zeros(len(y)), y, groups)):
        fold_ids[va] = k
    return fold_ids


def cross_fitted_meta(X, y, fold_ids, tag, cols_note=""):
    """Meta-learner trained on 4 folds of OOF rows, scored on the held-out fold."""
    pred = np.zeros(len(y), dtype=int)
    coefs = []
    for k in range(H.N_SPLITS):
        tr, va = H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
        sc = StandardScaler().fit(X[tr])          # scaler sees TRAIN rows only
        A, B = sc.transform(X[tr]).astype(np.float64), sc.transform(X[va]).astype(np.float64)
        clf = LogisticRegression(**META_KW).fit(A, y[tr])
        pred[va] = clf.predict(B)
        coefs.append({"fold": k, "n_meta_train": int(len(tr)),
                      "intercept": [round(float(v), 4) for v in clf.intercept_],
                      "coef": [round(float(v), 4) for v in clf.coef_[0]]})
    f1_pooled = float(H.f1_score(y, pred, average="macro", labels=list(H.CLASSES),
                                 zero_division=0))
    per_fold = []
    for k in range(H.N_SPLITS):
        _, va = H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
        per_fold.append({
            "fold": k, "n_val": int(len(va)),
            "f1": round(float(H.f1_score(y[va], pred[va], average="macro",
                                         labels=list(H.CLASSES), zero_division=0)), 6),
        })
    std = float(np.std([r["f1"] for r in per_fold]))
    print(f"{tag:34s} pooled OOF = {f1_pooled:.6f} | delta vs {H.BASELINE_OOF_F1:.6f} = "
          f"{f1_pooled - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1:+.6f} | fold std {std:.6f} {cols_note}")
    return {"tag": tag, "pooled_oof_macro_f1": f1_pooled,
            "delta_vs_baseline": f1_pooled - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1,
            "per_class_f1": H.per_class_f1(y, pred),
            "per_fold": per_fold, "fold_macro_f1_std": std,
            "fold_models": coefs, "feature_names": META_FEATURES[: X.shape[1]]}


def strict_nested_base(y, ids, clean_texts, fold_ids, oof_text, oof_id):
    """Double-nested base features: for meta-eval fold k, the meta-train rows
    get base predictions from models that saw NEITHER their own fold NOR k."""
    n = len(y)
    P_text = np.zeros((n, 3))
    P_id = np.zeros((n, 3))
    t0 = time.time()
    for k in range(H.N_SPLITS):
        _, va = H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
        # eval rows: base model trained on everything except fold k (= the EXP 0 OOF)
        P_text[va], P_id[va] = oof_text[va], oof_id[va]
        for j in range(H.N_SPLITS):
            if j == k:
                continue
            tr = np.flatnonzero((fold_ids != j) & (fold_ids != k))
            vj = np.flatnonzero(fold_ids == j)
            assert len(tr) + len(vj) + len(va) == n
            word, char = H.fit_vectorizers(clean_texts[tr])
            A = H.matrix_from(word, char, clean_texts[tr])
            B = H.matrix_from(word, char, clean_texts[vj])
            del word, char
            clf = H.fit_text_lr(A, y[tr])
            P_text[vj] = clf.predict_proba(B)
            del A, B, clf
            # restricted prior pool: no fold-k rows, no fold-j rows
            P_id[vj] = H.id_prior(ids[vj], ids[tr], y[tr], tag=f"strict_f{k}_f{j}")
        print(f"  strict fold{k} done ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s elapsed)")
    return P_text, P_id


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--text-source", default="lr", choices=["lr", "ens"])
    ap.add_argument("--skip-strict", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    t_start = time.time()

    H.banner("EXP 4 -- OOF meta-stacker (nested grouped CV)")
    d = H.load_data()
    y, ids, clean_texts, raw = d["y"], d["ids"], d["clean_texts"], d["train"]["text"].to_numpy()

    anchor = np.load(H.PRED_DIR / "v2_0_baseline_anchor_oof.npz")
    fold_ids, oof_id = anchor["fold_ids"], anchor["oof_id"]
    oof_text = anchor["oof_text"]
    if args.text_source == "ens":
        e = np.load(H.PRED_DIR / "v2_1_text_ensemble_oof.npz")
        oof_text, oof_id = e["oof_ens"], e["oof_id"]

    mf = meta_folds(y, clean_texts)
    assert np.array_equal(mf, fold_ids), "meta CV split != outer grouped split"
    print("meta-learner's own grouped CV split == outer grouped split (asserted)")

    Hraw = H.hand_feature_matrix(raw)
    print(f"text source: {args.text_source} | text-only OOF F1 = "
          f"{H.macro_f1(y, oof_text):.6f} | id-only = {H.macro_f1(y, oof_id):.6f} | "
          f"reference blend@0.40 = "
          f"{H.macro_f1(y, H.blend(oof_text, oof_id, H.ALPHA_BASELINE)):.6f}")

    specs = {
        "text+id+hand (spec)": np.hstack([oof_text, oof_id, Hraw]),
        "text+id (no hand)": np.hstack([oof_text, oof_id]),
        "text+hand (no id)": np.hstack([oof_text, Hraw]),
        "id+hand (no text)": np.hstack([oof_id, Hraw]),
    }
    results = []
    for tag, X in specs.items():
        results.append(cross_fitted_meta(X, y, fold_ids, f"[spec] {tag}"))

    strict = None
    if not args.skip_strict:
        print("\n-- strict double-nested base features (20 extra text fits) --")
        s_text, s_id = strict_nested_base(y, ids, clean_texts, fold_ids,
                                          oof_text, oof_id)
        X = np.hstack([s_text, s_id, Hraw])
        strict = cross_fitted_meta(X, y, fold_ids, "[strict] text+id+hand")
        strict["reference_blend_0.40_on_strict_base"] = round(
            H.macro_f1(y, H.blend(s_text, s_id, H.ALPHA_BASELINE)), 6)
        print(f"{'  [strict] reference blend@0.40':34s} = "
              f"{strict['reference_blend_0.40_on_strict_base']:.6f}")
        np.savez_compressed(H.CACHE_DIR / "strict_base_probs.npz",
                            oof_text=s_text, oof_id=s_id, fold_ids=fold_ids)

    headline = results[0]
    payload = {
        "experiment": "v2_4_meta_stacker",
        "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "python": sys.version.split()[0], "seed": H.SEED,
        "text_source": args.text_source,
        "validation": {"method": "StratifiedGroupKFold", "n_splits": H.N_SPLITS,
                       "shuffle": True, "random_state": H.SEED,
                       "group_key": "clean_text.lower().strip()",
                       "metric": "pooled OOF macro F1"},
        "meta_learner": {"model": "LogisticRegression", **META_KW,
                         "scaler": "StandardScaler fitted on meta-train rows only",
                         "features": META_FEATURES,
                         "note": "base features are the grouped OOF probabilities; "
                                 "meta-learner cross-fitted with its own grouped CV"},
        "results": results,
        "strict_nested": strict,
        "baseline_oof_f1": H.BASELINE_OOF_F1,
        "delta_vs_baseline_headline": headline["delta_vs_baseline"],
        "beats_baseline": bool(headline["delta_vs_baseline"] > 0),
        "runtime_seconds": round(time.time() - t_start, 1),
    }
    H.save_result("4_meta_stacker", payload)
    print(f"\nheadline (spec, text+id+hand): {headline['pooled_oof_macro_f1']:.6f} "
          f"| delta {headline['delta_vs_baseline']:+.6f} | beats baseline: "
          f"{payload['beats_baseline']} | runtime {payload['runtime_seconds']}s")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


============================================================
FILE: .\src\experiment_v2_5_combined.py
============================================================

"""
src/experiment_v2_5_combined.py
===============================

EXPERIMENT 5 (prompt priority 5)
--------------------------------
"Combine the winners and report the final combined OOF F1."

Gate first
----------
The prompt makes this step conditional on a single experiment beating the locked
baseline 0.639579. The gate is evaluated here on the HONEST number of each
experiment (a config whose weights/alpha were tuned on the same pooled OOF it is
scored on does not count as a winner; its cross-fitted counterpart does), and
compared against the fold noise scale (baseline per-fold std = 0.0038).

Combination, run honestly
-------------------------
Everything is evaluated with the cross-fitted protocol -- any free parameter is
fitted on the OOF rows of 4 folds and scored on the held-out 5th fold -- so every
row below is directly comparable to 0.639579. Combinations that mix several text
models are built by averaging already-computed leak-free OOF probability
matrices, so nothing is re-fitted and nothing leaks.

This script writes only reports/ and predictions/ artifacts. It does NOT build
any submission, and it never touches the locked baseline notebook or
submission_1.csv (sha1-verified).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

import experiment_v2_harness as H
import experiment_v2_3_per_class_alpha as E3

TOLERANCE = 0.005          # "similar range" band from the prompt's stop condition


def honest_per_class(p_text, p_id, y, fold_ids, tag, verbose=True):
    """Cross-fitted per-class blend weights: tune w on 4 folds, score the 5th."""
    pred = np.zeros(len(y), dtype=int)
    ws = {}
    for k in range(H.N_SPLITS):
        tr, va = H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
        w, _ = E3.nm_search(y[tr], p_text[tr], p_id[tr])
        pred[va] = H.blend_per_class(p_text[va], p_id[va], w).argmax(1)
        ws[k] = [round(float(v), 4) for v in w]
    f1_pooled = float(H.f1_score(y, pred, average="macro", labels=list(H.CLASSES),
                                 zero_division=0))
    per_fold, fixed_pred = [], H.blend(p_text, p_id, H.ALPHA_BASELINE).argmax(1)
    for k in range(H.N_SPLITS):
        _, va = H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
        per_fold.append({
            "fold": k, "n_val": int(len(va)),
            "f1_fixed_alpha_0.40": round(H.macro_f1(
                y[va], H.blend(p_text[va], p_id[va], H.ALPHA_BASELINE)), 6),
            "f1_per_class_crossfitted": round(float(H.f1_score(
                y[va], pred[va], average="macro", labels=list(H.CLASSES),
                zero_division=0)), 6),
            "w": ws[k],
        })
    f1_fixed = float(H.f1_score(y, fixed_pred, average="macro",
                                labels=list(H.CLASSES), zero_division=0))
    std = float(np.std([r["f1_per_class_crossfitted"] for r in per_fold]))
    std_fixed = float(np.std([r["f1_fixed_alpha_0.40"] for r in per_fold]))
    if verbose:
        print(f"{tag:52s} OOF={f1_pooled:.6f} (delta {f1_pooled - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1:+.6f})"
              f" | fixed-alpha same-protocol {f1_fixed:.6f} "
              f"(delta {f1_fixed - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1:+.6f}) | std {std:.6f} vs "
              f"{std_fixed:.6f}")
    return {
        "tag": tag, "pooled_oof_macro_f1": f1_pooled,
        "delta_vs_baseline": f1_pooled - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1,
        "per_class_f1": H.per_class_f1(y, pred),
        "per_fold": per_fold, "w_per_fold": ws,
        "fold_macro_f1_std": std,
        "fold_macro_f1_std_fixed_alpha": std_fixed,
        "fixed_alpha_0.40_same_protocol": f1_fixed,
    }


def cross_fitted_proba_matrix(p_text, p_id, w_per_fold):
    """Rebuild the cross-fitted blended probability matrix from the per-fold w."""
    proba = np.zeros((len(p_text), 3))
    for k_str, w in w_per_fold.items():
        k = int(k_str)
        _, va = H.fold_split(
            np.load(H.CACHE_DIR / "fold_ids.npy"), k)
        proba[va] = H.blend_per_class(p_text[va], p_id[va], w)
    return proba


def main() -> int:
    t_start = time.time()
    H.banner("EXP 5 -- combine the surviving candidates (cross-fitted protocol)")
    H.check_protected()

    d = H.load_data()
    y = d["y"]
    P = H.PRED_DIR
    a0 = np.load(P / "v2_0_baseline_anchor_oof.npz")
    oof_text, oof_id, fold_ids = a0["oof_text"], a0["oof_id"], a0["fold_ids"]
    e1 = np.load(P / "v2_1_text_ensemble_oof.npz")
    e2 = np.load(P / "v2_2_hand_features_oof.npz")
    m2 = json.loads((H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_2_hand_features_metrics.json").read_text())
    m3 = json.loads((H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_3_per_class_alpha_metrics.json").read_text())

    base_f1 = H.macro_f1(y, H.blend(oof_text, oof_id, H.ALPHA_BASELINE))
    base_std = float(np.std([H.macro_f1(y[va], H.blend(oof_text[va], oof_id[va],
                                                      H.ALPHA_BASELINE))
                             for _, va in (H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
                                           for k in range(H.N_SPLITS))]))
    print(f"locked baseline: OOF {base_f1:.6f} | per-fold std {base_std:.6f}")
    print(f"comparison band: a delta is only meaningful if it survives cross-fitting "
          f"AND exceeds the fold-noise scale (~{base_std:.4f})")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # GATE: honest delta of every single experiment
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    print("\n--- GATE: single-experiment honest deltas ---")
    single = {}
    r3ens = m3["results"]["ens"]
    single["v2_1_text_ensemble"] = {
        "honest_config": "equal-weight text ensemble, alpha re-gridded to "
                         f"{r3ens['single_alpha_best']['alpha']} on pooled OOF",
        "oof_macro_f1": r3ens["single_alpha_best"]["macro_f1"],
        "tuned_on_scored_oof": True,
        "cross_fitted_delta": r3ens["single_alpha_best"]["macro_f1"] - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1}
    s2 = m2["pooled_oof_macro_f1"]
    scales = list(m2["features"]["block_scales_swept"])
    best_s = max(scales, key=lambda s: s2[f"blend_scale{s}_0.40"])
    single["v2_2_hand_features"] = {
        "honest_config": f"hand features, best scale {best_s}, alpha fixed at 0.40 "
                         f"(alpha NOT tuned on the scored OOF)",
        "oof_macro_f1": s2[f"blend_scale{best_s}_0.40"],
        "tuned_on_scored_oof": False,
        "cross_fitted_delta": s2[f"blend_scale{best_s}_0.40"] - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1}
    r3lr = m3["results"]["lr"]["per_class_cross_fitted"]
    single["v2_3_per_class_alpha"] = {
        "honest_config": "per-class blend weights, cross-fitted (w tuned on 4 folds, "
                         "scored on the 5th)",
        "oof_macro_f1": r3lr["macro_f1"],
        "tuned_on_scored_oof": False,
        "cross_fitted_delta": r3lr["delta_vs_baseline"]}
    m4 = json.loads((H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_4_meta_stacker_metrics.json").read_text())
    single["v2_4_meta_stacker"] = {
        "honest_config": "meta-LR on [text_probs, id_probs, hand features], cross-fitted",
        "oof_macro_f1": m4["results"][0]["pooled_oof_macro_f1"],
        "tuned_on_scored_oof": False,
        "cross_fitted_delta": m4["results"][0]["delta_vs_baseline"]}
    for k, v in single.items():
        v["beats_baseline"] = bool(v["cross_fitted_delta"] > 0)
        v["delta_exceeds_fold_noise"] = bool(abs(v["cross_fitted_delta"]) > base_std)
        print(f"  {k:24s} OOF {v['oof_macro_f1']:.6f} | honest delta "
              f"{v['cross_fitted_delta']:+.6f} | beats={v['beats_baseline']} | "
              f"exceeds fold noise={v['delta_exceeds_fold_noise']}")

    survivors = [k for k, v in single.items()
                 if v["beats_baseline"] and v["delta_exceeds_fold_noise"]]
    gate_pass = len(survivors) > 0
    gate_note = (
        f"survivors exceeding both tests: {survivors or 'none'}; "
        + ("gate PASSED -> combination proceeds as a real candidate"
           if gate_pass else
           "gate NOT passed -> the combination below is reported for completeness "
           "only and does not qualify as beating the baseline"))
    print(f"\nGATE: {'PASS' if gate_pass else 'NOT PASSED'} -- {gate_note}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # COMBINATION (all cross-fitted => honest, directly comparable)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    print("\n--- COMBINATION (every free parameter cross-fitted) ---")
    p_lr = oof_text
    p_hand = e2["oof_scale0.1"] if "oof_scale0.1" in e2 else e2[f"oof_scale{scales[2]}"]
    p_ens = e1["oof_ens"]
    p_lr_hand = 0.5 * p_lr + 0.5 * p_hand
    p_all3 = (p_lr + p_hand + p_ens) / 3.0
    p_lr_ens = 0.5 * p_lr + 0.5 * p_ens

    combos = []
    combos.append(honest_per_class(p_lr, oof_id, y, fold_ids,
                                   "EXP3 survivor: per-class w on LR text + id prior"))
    combos.append(honest_per_class(p_hand, oof_id, y, fold_ids,
                                   "EXP2 text (hand scale 0.1) + per-class w + id prior"))
    combos.append(honest_per_class(p_lr_hand, oof_id, y, fold_ids,
                                   "COMBO: mean(LR, hand) text + per-class w + id prior"))
    combos.append(honest_per_class(p_lr_ens, oof_id, y, fold_ids,
                                   "COMBO: mean(LR, ensemble) text + per-class w + id prior"))
    combos.append(honest_per_class(p_all3, oof_id, y, fold_ids,
                                   "COMBO: mean(LR, hand, ensemble) text + per-class w + id prior"))
    # fixed-alpha versions of the same text sources, for a like-for-like view
    fixed = []
    text_sources = [("LR text (baseline)", p_lr), ("hand scale 0.1 text", p_hand),
                    ("mean(LR, hand)", p_lr_hand), ("mean(LR, ens)", p_lr_ens),
                    ("mean(LR, hand, ens)", p_all3)]
    for tag, p in text_sources:
        f1 = H.macro_f1(y, H.blend(p, oof_id, H.ALPHA_BASELINE))
        std = float(np.std([H.macro_f1(y[va], H.blend(p[va], oof_id[va], H.ALPHA_BASELINE))
                            for _, va in (H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
                                          for k in range(H.N_SPLITS))]))
        pb = H.blend(p, oof_id, H.ALPHA_BASELINE)
        fixed.append({"tag": f"fixed alpha 0.40 | {tag}", "pooled_oof_macro_f1": f1,
                      "delta_vs_baseline": f1 - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1,
                      "fold_macro_f1_std": std,
                      "per_fold_macro_f1": [round(H.macro_f1(
                          y[va], pb[va]), 6) for _, va in
                          (H.fold_split(fold_ids, k) for k in range(H.N_SPLITS))],
                      "per_class_f1": H.per_class_f1(y, pb.argmax(1))})
        print(f"{'fixed alpha 0.40 | ' + tag:52s} OOF={f1:.6f} "
              f"(delta {f1 - H.BASELINE_OOF_F1:+.6f}) | std {std:.6f}")

    best = max(combos, key=lambda r: r["pooled_oof_macro_f1"])
    # Tie-break: a fixed-alpha row is preferred over a cross-fitted one at equal
    # F1 (fewer tuned parameters), and the locked baseline sits at the top.
    def rank(r):
        return (round(r["pooled_oof_macro_f1"], 6),
                1 if r["tag"].startswith("fixed alpha") else 0)
    best_overall = max(combos + fixed, key=rank)
    print(f"\nbest cross-fitted combination: {best['tag']} -> "
          f"{best['pooled_oof_macro_f1']:.6f} "
          f"(delta {best['delta_vs_baseline']:+.6f})")
    print(f"best overall configuration     : {best_overall['tag']} -> "
          f"{best_overall['pooled_oof_macro_f1']:.6f} "
          f"(delta {best_overall['delta_vs_baseline']:+.6f})")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # stop conditions
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    print("\n--- STOP CONDITIONS ---")
    std_ok = abs(best["fold_macro_f1_std"] - base_std) <= TOLERANCE
    print(f"per-fold std {best['fold_macro_f1_std']:.6f} vs baseline {base_std:.6f} "
          f"-> |delta| {abs(best['fold_macro_f1_std'] - base_std):.6f} "
          f"<= {TOLERANCE}: {std_ok}")
    per_fold_delta = [round(b["f1_per_class_crossfitted"] - b["f1_fixed_alpha_0.40"], 6)
                      for b in best["per_fold"]]
    print("per-fold delta vs fixed alpha (no single-fold swing):", per_fold_delta)
    swings = [abs(v) for v in per_fold_delta]
    print(f"max |per-fold swing| {max(swings):.6f} "
          f"({'OK' if max(swings) < 0.02 else 'SUSPICIOUS'})")
    print(f"ID-prior leak-freedom re-verified: val ids disjoint from prior pool on all "
          f"5 folds, production prior == independent re-implementation "
          f"(max |diff| <= 1e-10) -- see reports/v2_0_baseline_anchor_metrics.json "
          f"-> id_prior_leak_proof")
    H.check_protected()
    print("no submission file was written by this script (by design)")

    best_is_fixed = best_overall["tag"].startswith("fixed alpha")
    best_config = {
        "name": best_overall["tag"],
        "oof_macro_f1": round(best_overall["pooled_oof_macro_f1"], 6),
        "delta_vs_baseline": round(best_overall["delta_vs_baseline"], 6),
        "per_class_f1": best_overall["per_class_f1"],
        "per_fold_std": round(best_overall["fold_macro_f1_std"], 6),
        "text_model": {
            "type": "LogisticRegression", **H.LR_KW,
            "features": "word TfidfVectorizer(analyzer=word, ngram_range=(1,2), "
                        "min_df=2, max_features=60000, sublinear_tf=True) + "
                        "char TfidfVectorizer(analyzer=char_wb, ngram_range=(3,5), "
                        "min_df=2, max_features=60000, sublinear_tf=True), hstacked",
            "cleaning": "URL -> ' URL ', @mention -> ' USER ', whitespace collapsed; "
                        "no lowercasing, no stemming",
            "source": "LR text only" if best_is_fixed and best_overall is fixed[0]
                      else "see name",
        },
        "id_prior": {"k": H.K_NEIGHBORS, "weights": "1/(distance+1e-6)",
                     "pool": "training-fold ids/labels only (leak-free, asserted per fold)",
                     "note": "self-matches excluded; queries disjoint from the pool"},
        "blend": {
            "form": "P(c) = a * text_probs_c + (1 - a) * id_probs_c",
            "alpha": H.ALPHA_BASELINE if best_is_fixed else None,
            "cross_fitted_w_per_fold": (None if best_is_fixed
                                        else best_overall["w_per_fold"]),
            "tuned_parameters": ("none beyond the baseline's own choice of alpha=0.40"
                                 if best_is_fixed else "one 3-vector per fold"),
        },
        "duplicate_override": "unanimous cleaned-text lookup, conflicting groups skipped",
        "protocol": {"validation": "StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)",
                     "group_key": "clean_text.lower().strip()",
                     "metric": "pooled leak-free OOF Macro F1",
                     "seed": H.SEED},
        "per_fold_macro_f1": best_overall.get(
            "per_fold_macro_f1",
            [r["f1_per_class_crossfitted"] for r in best_overall["per_fold"]]
            if "per_fold" in best_overall else None),
        "status": "best validated config found by experiment_v2_*; NOT submitted "
                  "(submission_2 requires an explicit request)",
    }

    payload = {
        "experiment": "v2_5_combined",
        "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "python": sys.version.split()[0], "seed": H.SEED,
        "validation": {"method": "StratifiedGroupKFold", "n_splits": H.N_SPLITS,
                       "shuffle": True, "random_state": H.SEED,
                       "group_key": "clean_text.lower().strip()",
                       "metric": "pooled OOF macro F1"},
        "baseline_oof_f1": H.BASELINE_OOF_F1,
        "baseline_per_fold_std": base_std,
        "gate": {"per_experiment": single, "survivors": survivors,
                 "any_single_experiment_beat_baseline": gate_pass,
                 "note": gate_note},
        "cross_fitted_combinations": combos,
        "fixed_alpha_combinations": fixed,
        "best": best_overall,
        "best_cross_fitted": best,
        "stop_conditions": {
            "per_fold_std_within_tolerance": bool(std_ok),
            "tolerance": TOLERANCE,
            "best_std": best["fold_macro_f1_std"],
            "baseline_std": base_std,
            "per_fold_delta_vs_fixed_alpha": per_fold_delta,
            "max_abs_per_fold_swing": max(swings),
            "id_prior_leak_proof": "re-verified (val ids disjoint from pool on all 5 "
                                   "folds; prod == independent re-implementation)",
        },
        "best_config": best_config,
        "submission_written": False,
        "runtime_seconds": round(time.time() - t_start, 1),
        "_oof_arrays": {
            "oof_best": cross_fitted_proba_matrix(p_lr, oof_id, best["w_per_fold"])
            if best["tag"].startswith("EXP3") else np.eye(3)[
                H.blend_per_class(p_lr, oof_id, best["w_per_fold"][0]).argmax(1)],
            "fold_ids": fold_ids},
    }
    H.save_result("5_combined", payload)
    H.save_config("best_validated_config", best_config)
    H.record_comparison("v2_5_combined_best",
                        f"EXP 5 combination: {best_overall['tag']} "
                        f"(gate {'PASS' if gate_pass else 'NOT PASSED'})",
                        best_overall["pooled_oof_macro_f1"],
                        best_overall["per_class_f1"],
                        best_overall["fold_macro_f1_std"], best_config,
                        note=gate_note)
    print(f"\nmetrics -> reports/v2_5_combined_metrics.json | "
          f"best config -> reports/v2_configs/best_validated_config.json | "
          f"runtime {payload['runtime_seconds']}s")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


============================================================
FILE: .\src\experiment_v2_harness.py
============================================================

"""
src/experiment_v2_harness.py
============================

Shared harness for the `src/experiment_v2_*.py` family. Goal: try to beat the
locked, validated baseline (pooled grouped-fold leak-free OOF Macro F1 =
0.639579, alpha = 0.40, k = 20) WITHOUT touching

    uap_submission/reproducible/submission_reproducible_file_1.ipynb
    uap_submission/reproducible/submission/submission_1.csv

Hard rules honoured here (carried over verbatim from the baseline):

* Validation protocol is fixed: StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True,
  random_state=42) with group = cleaned + lowercased text. Never plain
  StratifiedKFold.
* Any ID-locality feature is leak-free: a query row's own label is never in the
  prior pool. This is asserted per fold (val ids disjoint from train ids) and
  the prior function is independently re-implemented and diffed.
* The reported metric is always pooled OOF Macro F1 over the 5 grouped folds,
  directly comparable to 0.639579.
* CPU only, no network, no external data, no pretrained weights.
* SEED = 42 everywhere.

Nothing in this file is imported by the locked notebook; the baseline is left
byte-identical. This module only *re-implements* the baseline primitives so a
new experiment can reuse them, and caches the per-fold TF-IDF matrices so
several experiments share one vectorisation pass.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import scipy.sparse as sp
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
from sklearn.metrics import f1_score
from sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedGroupKFold
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Constants (identical to the locked baseline)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
SEED = 42
N_SPLITS = 5
ALPHA_GRID = [round(0.05 * i, 2) for i in range(21)]
ALPHA_BASELINE = 0.40
K_NEIGHBORS, ID_EPS = 20, 1e-6
CLASSES = (0, 1, 2)

BASELINE_OOF_F1 = 0.639579          # the number every experiment is compared to

WORD_KW = dict(analyzer="word", ngram_range=(1, 2), min_df=2, max_features=60000,
               sublinear_tf=True, dtype=np.float32)
CHAR_KW = dict(analyzer="char_wb", ngram_range=(3, 5), min_df=2, max_features=60000,
               sublinear_tf=True, dtype=np.float32)
LR_KW = dict(solver="lbfgs", C=4.0, class_weight="balanced", max_iter=2000,
             random_state=SEED)

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
CACHE_DIR = ROOT / "cache" / "v2"
PRED_DIR = ROOT / "predictions" / "v2"
REPORTS_DIR = ROOT / "reports"
LOGS_DIR = ROOT / "logs"

URL_RE = re.compile(r"(?:https?://|www\.)\S+", flags=re.IGNORECASE)
MENTION_RE = re.compile(r"@\w+")
WS_RE = re.compile(r"\s+", flags=re.UNICODE)
REPEAT_RE = re.compile(r"(.)\1{2,}", flags=re.UNICODE)
HASH_RE = re.compile(r"#\w+", flags=re.UNICODE)

BN_LO, BN_HI = 0x0980, 0x09FF
EMOJI_RANGES = (
    (0x1F300, 0x1FAFF), (0x2600, 0x27BF), (0x1F000, 0x1F2FF),
    (0xFE00, 0xFE0F), (0x1F1E6, 0x1F1FF), (0x2190, 0x21FF),
)
PUNCT = set("!\"#$%&'()*+,-./:;<=>?@[\\]^_`{|}~")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Text cleaning -- byte-identical logic to the baseline notebook
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def clean_text(text):
    # URL -> ' URL ', @mention -> ' USER ', collapse whitespace. Nothing else.
    s = "" if text is None else str(text)
    s = URL_RE.sub(" URL ", s)
    s = MENTION_RE.sub(" USER ", s)
    return WS_RE.sub(" ", s).strip()


def group_key(clean):
    # Grouping key: cleaned text, lowercased and stripped.
    return str(clean).lower().strip()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Data loading
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def load_data(verbose: bool = True):
    train = pd.read_csv(ROOT / "train.csv", encoding="utf-8")
    test = pd.read_csv(ROOT / "test.csv", encoding="utf-8")
    sample = pd.read_csv(ROOT / "sample_submission.csv", encoding="utf-8")
    assert list(train.columns) == ["id", "text", "y"], list(train.columns)
    assert list(test.columns) == ["id", "text"], list(test.columns)
    assert list(sample.columns) == ["id", "y_pred"], list(sample.columns)

    for df in (train, test):
        df["text"] = df["text"].astype("string").fillna("").astype(str)
    train["clean"] = train["text"].map(clean_text)
    test["clean"] = test["text"].map(clean_text)

    y = train["y"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
    ids = train["id"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int64)
    test_ids = test["id"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int64)
    clean_texts = train["clean"].to_numpy()      # OBJECT array, never asarray(list)
    test_clean = test["clean"].to_numpy()

    assert set(map(int, np.unique(y))) <= set(CLASSES)
    assert len(set(ids) & set(test_ids)) == 0, "train/test ids must not overlap"
    assert len(sample) == len(test)
    if verbose:
        print(f"train {train.shape} | test {test.shape} | sample {sample.shape}")
        print("class counts:", {int(c): int((y == c).sum()) for c in CLASSES})
    return dict(train=train, test=test, sample=sample, y=y, ids=ids,
                test_ids=test_ids, clean_texts=clean_texts, test_clean=test_clean)


def sha1_file(path: Path, block: int = 1 << 20) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha1()
    with open(path, "rb") as fh:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(block), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def input_checksums() -> dict:
    return {name: {"sha1": sha1_file(ROOT / name),
                   "bytes": (ROOT / name).stat().st_size}
            for name in ("train.csv", "test.csv", "sample_submission.csv")}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Fold construction -- StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def build_folds(clean_texts, y, verbose: bool = True) -> np.ndarray:
    keys = pd.Index([group_key(t) for t in clean_texts], dtype="object")
    groups = pd.factorize(keys, sort=False)[0]
    sizes = pd.Series(groups).value_counts()
    n_groups, n_dup = int(len(sizes)), int((sizes > 1).sum())
    rows_in_dup = int(sizes[sizes > 1].sum())

    sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=N_SPLITS, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)
    fold_ids = np.full(len(y), -1, dtype=int)
    for k, (_tr, va) in enumerate(sgkf.split(np.zeros(len(y)), y, groups)):
        fold_ids[va] = k
    assert (fold_ids >= 0).all(), "some rows received no fold id"

    crossing = pd.DataFrame({"g": groups, "f": fold_ids}).groupby("g")["f"].nunique()
    n_crossing = int((crossing > 1).sum())
    assert n_crossing == 0, f"{n_crossing} text groups span >1 fold -> leakage"
    if verbose:
        print(f"groups: {n_groups} | duplicate groups: {n_dup} | "
              f"rows in duplicate groups: {rows_in_dup} | groups spanning >1 fold: 0")
    return fold_ids


def fold_split(fold_ids: np.ndarray, k: int):
    va = np.flatnonzero(fold_ids == k)
    tr = np.flatnonzero(fold_ids != k)
    return tr, va


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# ID-locality prior -- VERBATIM copy of the baseline notebook function
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def id_prior(query_ids, train_ids, train_labels, k=K_NEIGHBORS, eps=ID_EPS,
             n_classes=3, tag="prior", assert_disjoint=True):
    # k-nearest-id label prior (vectorised). Only TRAIN ids/labels are passed in,
    # so a validation/test row's own label can never influence its own prediction.
    q = np.asarray(query_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    t = np.asarray(train_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    yy = np.asarray(train_labels, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    assert t.size == yy.size and t.size == np.unique(t).size
    order = np.argsort(t, kind="stable")           # ascending id -> deterministic ties
    s_ids, s_y = t[order], yy[order]
    n, kk = len(s_ids), min(k, len(s_ids))

    pos = np.searchsorted(s_ids, q, side="left")
    cand = pos[:, None] + np.arange(-k, k + 1)[None, :]      # k left + k right
    valid = (cand >= 0) & (cand < n)
    cc = np.clip(cand, 0, n - 1)
    d = np.abs(s_ids[cc] - q[:, None]).astype(np.float64)
    d = np.where(valid & (s_ids[cc] != q[:, None]), d, np.inf)   # no self-match
    sel = np.argsort(d, axis=1, kind="stable")[:, :kk]           # exact k nearest
    dsel = np.take_along_axis(d, sel, axis=1)
    ysel = s_y[cc[np.arange(len(q))[:, None], sel]]

    good = np.isfinite(dsel)
    w = np.where(good, 1.0 / (np.where(good, dsel, 0.0) + eps), 0.0)
    P = np.stack([(w * (ysel == c)).sum(axis=1) for c in range(n_classes)], axis=1)
    tot = P.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    P = P / np.where(tot > 0, tot, 1.0)
    P[tot.ravel() <= 0] = 1.0 / n_classes
    overlap = int(np.isin(q, s_ids).sum())
    if overlap:
        print(f"WARNING {tag}: {overlap} query ids also in pool -> self-matches excluded")
    if assert_disjoint:
        assert overlap == 0, "leak: query ids present in the prior pool"
    return P


def id_prior_diagnostics(query_ids, train_ids, train_labels, k=K_NEIGHBORS,
                          eps=ID_EPS, tag="prior") -> dict:
    q = np.asarray(query_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    t = np.asarray(train_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    yy = np.asarray(train_labels, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    order = np.argsort(t, kind="stable")
    s_ids, s_y = t[order], yy[order]
    kk = int(min(k, len(s_ids)))
    pos = np.searchsorted(s_ids, q, side="left")
    cand = pos[:, None] + np.arange(-k, k + 1)[None, :]
    valid = (cand >= 0) & (cand < len(s_ids))
    cc = np.clip(cand, 0, len(s_ids) - 1)
    d = np.abs(s_ids[cc] - q[:, None]).astype(np.float64)
    d = np.where(valid & (s_ids[cc] != q[:, None]), d, np.inf)
    sel = np.argsort(d, axis=1, kind="stable")[:, :kk]
    ds = np.take_along_axis(d, sel, axis=1)
    used = np.isfinite(ds)
    return {
        "tag": tag, "n_queries": int(len(q)), "n_pool": int(len(s_ids)), "k": int(k),
        "weights": f"1/(distance+{eps})",
        "pool_ids_disjoint_from_queries": bool(np.isin(q, s_ids).sum() == 0),
        "self_matches_excluded": int(used.sum()),
        "mean_neighbours_used": round(float(used.sum(axis=1).mean()), 4),
        "queries_with_fewer_than_k": int((used.sum(axis=1) < kk).sum()),
        "queries_with_no_neighbour": int((used.sum(axis=1) == 0).sum()),
        "mean_kth_distance": (round(float(np.sort(np.where(used, ds, np.inf), axis=1)
                                    [:, -1].mean()), 4)
                             if len(q) else None),
    }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# INDEPENDENT re-implementation used only to DIFF the prior above.
# Deliberately a different algorithm: full distance matrix + full argsort, no
# searchsorted window, no self-exclusion-by-index -- processed in blocks.
# (Same approach the Part-1 re-validation used.)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def independent_knn_id_prior(query_ids, train_ids, train_labels, k=K_NEIGHBORS,
                             eps=ID_EPS, n_classes=3, block=300) -> np.ndarray:
    q = np.asarray(query_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    t = np.asarray(train_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    y = np.asarray(train_labels, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    order = np.argsort(t, kind="stable")
    s_ids, s_y = t[order], y[order]
    kk = int(min(k, len(s_ids)))
    out = np.empty((len(q), n_classes), dtype=float)
    inf = np.iinfo(np.int64).max
    for start in range(0, len(q), block):
        chunk = q[start:start + block]
        d = np.abs(chunk[:, None] - s_ids[None, :])
        d = np.where(chunk[:, None] == s_ids[None, :], inf, d)   # never pick self
        idx = np.argsort(d, axis=1, kind="stable")[:, :kk]       # naive full sort
        ds = np.take_along_axis(d, idx, axis=1).astype(np.float64)
        ys = s_y[idx]
        good = ds < inf
        w = np.where(good, 1.0 / (np.where(good, ds, 0.0) + eps), 0.0)
        blk = np.stack([(w * (ys == c)).sum(axis=1) for c in range(n_classes)], axis=1)
        tot = blk.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        blk = blk / np.where(tot > 0, tot, 1.0)
        blk[tot.ravel() <= 0] = 1.0 / n_classes
        out[start:start + len(chunk)] = blk
    return out


def prove_id_prior_leak_free(clean_texts, y, ids, fold_ids, tol=1e-10,
                             verbose=True):
    """Per fold: assert val ids disjoint from the prior pool, and diff the
    production prior against the independent re-implementation."""
    rows = []
    for k in range(N_SPLITS):
        tr, va = fold_split(fold_ids, k)
        overlap = int(np.intersect1d(ids[va], ids[tr]).size)
        assert overlap == 0, f"fold{k}: val ids present in prior pool"
        p_prod = id_prior(ids[va], ids[tr], y[tr], tag=f"fold{k} prior")
        p_ind = independent_knn_id_prior(ids[va], ids[tr], y[tr], k=K_NEIGHBORS)
        max_diff = float(np.abs(p_prod - p_ind).max())
        assert max_diff <= tol, f"fold{k}: prior differs from independent impl {max_diff}"
        diag = id_prior_diagnostics(ids[va], ids[tr], y[tr], tag=f"fold{k}_val")
        diag.update({"fold": k, "train_val_id_overlap": overlap,
                     "max_abs_diff_vs_independent": max_diff})
        rows.append(diag)
        if verbose:
            print(f"fold{k}: val&pool={overlap} "
                  f"max|prod-ind|={max_diff:.2e} "
                  f"mean_neighbours={diag['mean_neighbours_used']}")
    return rows


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# TF-IDF: per-fold vectorisers, cached to disk so experiments share one pass
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _cache_path(k: int, part: str, which: str) -> Path:
    return CACHE_DIR / f"fold{k}_{part}_{which}.npz"


def fold_tfidf(clean_texts, fold_ids, k, verbose=True, use_cache=True):
    """Fit word+char TF-IDF on fold k's TRAINING rows only and transform both
    splits. Returns (Xtr, Xva) as a single CSR float32 matrix (word|char)."""
    if use_cache:
        ctr, cva = _cache_path(k, "tfidf", "tr"), _cache_path(k, "tfidf", "va")
        if ctr.exists() and cva.exists():
            Xtr = sp.load_npz(ctr)
            Xva = sp.load_npz(cva)
            if verbose:
                print(f"fold{k}: tfidf from cache Xtr={Xtr.shape} Xva={Xva.shape}")
            return Xtr, Xva

    tr, va = fold_split(fold_ids, k)
    t0 = time.time()
    word = TfidfVectorizer(**WORD_KW)
    word.fit(clean_texts[tr])
    char = TfidfVectorizer(**CHAR_KW)
    char.fit(clean_texts[tr])
    Xtr = sp.hstack([word.transform(clean_texts[tr]),
                     char.transform(clean_texts[tr])], format="csr").astype(np.float32)
    Xva = sp.hstack([word.transform(clean_texts[va]),
                     char.transform(clean_texts[va])], format="csr").astype(np.float32)
    del word, char
    if use_cache:
        CACHE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        sp.save_npz(_cache_path(k, "tfidf", "tr"), Xtr, compressed=False)
        sp.save_npz(_cache_path(k, "tfidf", "va"), Xva, compressed=False)
    if verbose:
        print(f"fold{k}: tfidf fit {time.time() - t0:.1f}s "
              f"Xtr={Xtr.shape} nnz={Xtr.nnz:,} Xva={Xva.shape} nnz={Xva.nnz:,}")
    return Xtr, Xva


def fold_tfidf_word_only(clean_texts, fold_ids, k, verbose=True, use_cache=True):
    """Word TF-IDF only (needed by ComplementNB in experiment 1)."""
    if use_cache:
        ctr, cva = _cache_path(k, "word", "tr"), _cache_path(k, "word", "va")
        if ctr.exists() and cva.exists():
            return sp.load_npz(ctr), sp.load_npz(cva)
    tr, va = fold_split(fold_ids, k)
    t0 = time.time()
    word = TfidfVectorizer(**WORD_KW)
    word.fit(clean_texts[tr])
    Xtr = word.transform(clean_texts[tr]).astype(np.float32)
    Xva = word.transform(clean_texts[va]).astype(np.float32)
    if use_cache:
        CACHE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        sp.save_npz(_cache_path(k, "word", "tr"), Xtr, compressed=False)
        sp.save_npz(_cache_path(k, "word", "va"), Xva, compressed=False)
    if verbose:
        print(f"fold{k}: word-only tfidf fit {time.time() - t0:.1f}s "
              f"Xtr={Xtr.shape} Xva={Xva.shape}")
    return Xtr, Xva


def fit_vectorizers(texts):
    """Fit the word + char TF-IDF pair on an arbitrary row subset."""
    word = TfidfVectorizer(**WORD_KW)
    word.fit(texts)
    char = TfidfVectorizer(**CHAR_KW)
    char.fit(texts)
    return word, char


def matrix_from(word, char, texts):
    return sp.hstack([word.transform(texts), char.transform(texts)],
                     format="csr").astype(np.float32)


def fit_text_lr(X, y, **kw):
    """The baseline text classifier: LogisticRegression(C=4.0, balanced)."""
    from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
    params = dict(LR_KW)
    params.update(kw)
    return LogisticRegression(**params).fit(X, y)


def full_tfidf(clean_texts, test_clean, verbose=True, use_cache=True):
    """Word+char TF-IDF fitted on the FULL training set, applied to train+test."""
    if use_cache:
        ctr = _cache_path("full", "tfidf", "tr")
        cte = _cache_path("full", "tfidf", "te")
        if ctr.exists() and cte.exists():
            if verbose:
                print("full: tfidf from cache")
            return sp.load_npz(ctr), sp.load_npz(cte)
    t0 = time.time()
    word = TfidfVectorizer(**WORD_KW)
    word.fit(clean_texts)
    char = TfidfVectorizer(**CHAR_KW)
    char.fit(clean_texts)
    Xtr = sp.hstack([word.transform(clean_texts), char.transform(clean_texts)],
                    format="csr").astype(np.float32)
    Xte = sp.hstack([word.transform(test_clean), char.transform(test_clean)],
                    format="csr").astype(np.float32)
    n_w, n_c = len(word.vocabulary_), len(char.vocabulary_)
    del word, char
    if use_cache:
        CACHE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        sp.save_npz(ctr, Xtr, compressed=False)
        sp.save_npz(cte, Xte, compressed=False)
    if verbose:
        print(f"full: tfidf fit {time.time() - t0:.1f}s Xtr={Xtr.shape} "
              f"nnz={Xtr.nnz:,} word={n_w:,} char={n_c:,}")
    return Xtr, Xte


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Experiment 2: hand-crafted dense features (computed on the RAW text)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
HAND_FEATURE_NAMES = [
    "bangla_char_ratio", "latin_char_ratio", "digit_ratio",
    "punctuation_density", "repeated_char_run_max", "emoji_count",
    "has_url", "has_mention", "text_length",
]


def _is_emoji(cp: int) -> bool:
    return any(lo <= cp <= hi for lo, hi in EMOJI_RANGES)


def _row_hand_features(s: str) -> list:
    n = len(s)
    if n == 0:
        return [0.0] * len(HAND_FEATURE_NAMES)
    n_bn = n_lat = n_dig = n_pun = n_emo = 0
    for ch in s:
        cp = ord(ch)
        if BN_LO <= cp <= BN_HI:
            n_bn += 1
        elif ("a" <= ch <= "z") or ("A" <= ch <= "Z"):
            n_lat += 1
        elif "0" <= ch <= "9":
            n_dig += 1
        elif ch in PUNCT:
            n_pun += 1
        if _is_emoji(cp):
            n_emo += 1
    runs = [len(m.group(0)) for m in REPEAT_RE.finditer(s)]
    max_run = int(max(runs)) if runs else 0
    return [
        n_bn / n,
        n_lat / n,
        n_dig / n,
        n_pun / n,
        float(max_run),
        float(n_emo),
        1.0 if URL_RE.search(s) else 0.0,
        1.0 if MENTION_RE.search(s) else 0.0,
        float(n),
    ]


def hand_feature_matrix(raw_texts) -> np.ndarray:
    """Unscaled 9-column hand-crafted block (row-local: no cross-row info)."""
    return np.asarray([_row_hand_features("" if t is None else str(t))
                       for t in raw_texts], dtype=np.float64)


def hand_features(raw_texts, scaler=None, fit: bool = False):
    """9 hand-crafted row features -> float32 dense block (StandardScaler'd).

    `fit=True` fits a fresh StandardScaler on these rows (used on the training
    split of a fold only, so the scaler never sees validation rows).
    """
    D = hand_feature_matrix(raw_texts)
    if fit or scaler is None:
        scaler = StandardScaler().fit(D)
    Z = scaler.transform(D).astype(np.float32)
    return Z, scaler


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Metrics / blending
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def macro_f1(y_true, proba) -> float:
    return float(f1_score(y_true, np.asarray(proba).argmax(axis=1),
                          average="macro", labels=list(CLASSES), zero_division=0))


def per_class_f1(y_true, pred) -> dict:
    pred = np.asarray(pred)
    vals = f1_score(np.asarray(y_true), pred, average=None,
                    labels=list(CLASSES), zero_division=0)
    return {f"f1_class{int(c)}": round(float(v), 6) for c, v in zip(CLASSES, vals)}


def accuracy(y_true, proba) -> float:
    return float((np.asarray(proba).argmax(axis=1) == np.asarray(y_true)).mean())


def blend(p_text, p_id, alpha):
    p = alpha * np.asarray(p_text, dtype=float) + (1.0 - alpha) * np.asarray(p_id, dtype=float)
    return p / p.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)


def blend_per_class(p_text, p_id, w):
    """Per-class convex blend: col c uses w[c] for text and 1 - w[c] for id."""
    p_text = np.asarray(p_text, dtype=float)
    p_id = np.asarray(p_id, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float).reshape(1, -1)
    p = w * p_text + (1.0 - w) * p_id
    return p / p.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)


def alpha_grid(y, p_text, p_id):
    return [(a, macro_f1(y, blend(p_text, p_id, a))) for a in ALPHA_GRID]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Duplicate-text override (verbatim baseline behaviour, for parity checks)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def unanimous_lookup(texts, labels):
    df = pd.DataFrame({"t": pd.Series(list(texts), dtype="object"),
                       "y": np.asarray(labels)})
    g = df.groupby("t")["y"].agg(["nunique", "first"])
    uni = g[g["nunique"] == 1]
    lookup = {str(t): int(v) for t, v in uni["first"].items()}
    n_conflicting = int((g["nunique"] > 1).sum())
    return lookup, n_conflicting


def apply_override(texts, pred, lookup):
    out = np.asarray(pred).copy()
    hits = 0
    for i, t in enumerate(texts):
        v = lookup.get(str(t))
        if v is not None:
            out[i] = v
            hits += 1
    return out, hits


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Result IO
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
COMPARISON_CSV = REPORTS_DIR / "v2_comparison_table.csv"
CONFIGS_DIR = REPORTS_DIR / "v2_configs"
COMPARISON_COLUMNS = [
    "experiment", "description", "oof_macro_f1", "delta_vs_baseline",
    "per_class_f1", "per_fold_std", "config_hyperparams",
]

# The live, best-known-good submission. READ-ONLY: never touched, never written.
PROTECTED = {
    "uap_submission/reproducible/submission_reproducible_file_1.ipynb":
        "fa0e055fc226b20f6e0c366d9909ce4e813e33bb",
    "uap_submission/reproducible/submission/submission_1.csv":
        "cd623b33101873a3e22a19e7ad44197d791d1b4f",
    "train.csv": "66c6b109c8fd187f4c2c474a09159905d25978cd",
    "test.csv": "9caf35ded5a6fe4b2765cdc3340f192d0994d072",
    "sample_submission.csv": "73345c6143ad4e155bac35de1bf8d5d81ed1d039",
}


def check_protected(verbose: bool = True) -> dict:
    """Assert the locked baseline notebook/submission and the three input CSVs
    are byte-identical to their recorded sha1 (they are never written by any
    experiment_v2_* file)."""
    out = {}
    for rel, want in PROTECTED.items():
        p = ROOT / rel
        got = sha1_file(p) if p.exists() else "MISSING"
        out[rel] = {"expected_sha1": want, "actual_sha1": got, "match": got == want}
    bad = [k for k, v in out.items() if not v["match"]]
    if verbose:
        for k, v in out.items():
            print(f"protected {k}: {'unchanged' if v['match'] else 'CHANGED!'}")
    assert not bad, f"protected files modified: {bad}"
    return out


def save_result(name: str, payload: dict):
    PRED_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    REPORTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (REPORTS_DIR / f"v2_{name}_metrics.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(payload, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
    oof = payload.pop("_oof_arrays", None)
    if oof:
        np.savez_compressed(PRED_DIR / f"v2_{name}_oof.npz", **oof)
    return payload


def save_config(name: str, config: dict):
    """Persist the fitted config so a winner can be re-materialised later
    without repeating the search."""
    CONFIGS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = CONFIGS_DIR / f"{name}.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def record_comparison(experiment: str, description: str, oof_macro_f1: float,
                      per_class_f1, per_fold_std, config: dict,
                      note: str = "") -> dict:
    """Upsert one row into the single running reports/v2_comparison_table.csv and
    drop the config JSON next to it. Keyed on `experiment`, so re-running is
    idempotent and no history is lost."""
    REPORTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    CONFIGS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    full = dict(config)
    if note:
        full["evaluation_note"] = note
    row = {
        "experiment": experiment,
        "description": description,
        "oof_macro_f1": round(float(oof_macro_f1), 6),
        "delta_vs_baseline": round(float(oof_macro_f1) - BASELINE_OOF_F1, 6),
        "per_class_f1": json.dumps(per_class_f1, sort_keys=True),
        "per_fold_std": round(float(per_fold_std), 6),
        "config_hyperparams": json.dumps(config, sort_keys=True, default=str),
    }
    rows = []
    if COMPARISON_CSV.exists():
        rows = pd.read_csv(COMPARISON_CSV, dtype=str).to_dict("records")
    rows = [r for r in rows if r.get("experiment") != experiment]
    rows.append(row)
    tmp = COMPARISON_CSV.with_suffix(".csv.tmp")
    pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=COMPARISON_COLUMNS).to_csv(tmp, index=False)
    tmp.replace(COMPARISON_CSV)
    cfg_path = save_config(experiment, config)
    print(f"[comparison] {experiment}: OOF {oof_macro_f1:.6f} "
          f"(delta {row['delta_vs_baseline']:+.6f}) -> {COMPARISON_CSV.name}, "
          f"config -> {cfg_path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()}")
    return row


def read_comparison() -> pd.DataFrame:
    if not COMPARISON_CSV.exists():
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=COMPARISON_COLUMNS)
    return pd.read_csv(COMPARISON_CSV, dtype=str)


def banner(title: str):
    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print(title)
    print("=" * 78)


============================================================
FILE: .\src\experiment_v2_report.py
============================================================

"""
src/experiment_v2_report.py
===========================

Rebuilds the single running comparison table `reports/v2_comparison_table.csv`
and the per-experiment config registry `reports/v2_configs/*.json` from the
artifacts already on disk:

* `reports/v2_*_metrics.json`      -- every experiment's full result payload
* `predictions/v2/*_oof.npz`       -- every experiment's OOF probability matrices
* `cache/v2/strict_base_probs.npz` -- EXP 4's double-nested base probabilities

Rows are keyed on `experiment`, so re-running this script is idempotent: nothing
is duplicated and the full history of what was tried (wins AND losses) is
preserved. Every number is recomputed here from the cached OOF matrices, so the
table is consistent by construction rather than hand-copied.

Every row is scored on the SAME protocol: pooled leak-free OOF Macro F1 over
StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42), group =
clean_text.lower().strip(), compared against the locked baseline 0.639579.
Rows whose weights/alpha were chosen by looking at the same pooled OOF are
labelled `optimistic` -- their honest cross-fitted counterpart is a separate row.

This script never writes a submission and never touches the locked baseline
notebook / submission_1.csv (checked via sha1 at the end).

Usage:  python src/experiment_v2_report.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

import experiment_v2_harness as H

METRICS = {
    "v2_0_baseline_anchor": "baseline anchor: LR(C=4,balanced) text + k=20 ID prior, "
                            "blend alpha=0.40, grouped leak-free OOF (reproduces "
                            "0.639579 exactly)",
    "v2_1_text_ensemble": "text model replaced by equal-weight prob average of "
                          "LR + CalibratedClassifierCV(LinearSVC) + ComplementNB(word)",
    "v2_2_hand_features": "9 hand-crafted dense features (StandardScaler'd, fitted on "
                          "train rows only) hstacked onto word+char TF-IDF, LR refitted",
    "v2_3_per_class_alpha": "per-class 3-vector blend weights instead of a single alpha "
                            "(Nelder-Mead + local grid)",
    "v2_4_meta_stacker": "OOF meta-stacker: LR on [text_probs, id_probs, hand features], "
                         "cross-fitted with the same grouped 5-fold split",
    "v2_5_combined": "combination of the surviving candidates, cross-fitted",
}

PROTOCOL = {"validation": "StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)",
            "group_key": "clean_text.lower().strip()",
            "metric": "pooled leak-free OOF Macro F1",
            "baseline_oof_macro_f1": H.BASELINE_OOF_F1}


def pooled(y, proba):
    return H.macro_f1(y, proba)


def fold_scores(y, proba, fold_ids):
    vals = [H.macro_f1(y[va], proba[va]) for _, va in
            (H.fold_split(fold_ids, k) for k in range(H.N_SPLITS))]
    return vals, float(np.std(vals))


def add(experiment, description, y, proba, fold_ids, config, note=""):
    vals, std = fold_scores(y, proba, fold_ids)
    f1 = pooled(y, proba)
    full = dict(config)
    full.update(PROTOCOL)
    if note:
        full["evaluation_note"] = note
    full["per_fold_macro_f1"] = [round(v, 6) for v in vals]
    H.record_comparison(experiment, description, f1,
                        H.per_class_f1(y, proba.argmax(1)), std, full)
    return f1, std, vals


def main() -> int:
    H.banner("v2 comparison table rebuild (from cached OOF matrices + metrics JSON)")
    H.check_protected()

    d = H.load_data()
    y = d["y"]

    P = H.PRED_DIR
    need = ["v2_0_baseline_anchor_oof.npz", "v2_1_text_ensemble_oof.npz",
            "v2_2_hand_features_oof.npz", "v2_4_meta_stacker",
            "strict_base_probs.npz"]
    missing = [n for n in need[:-1]
               if not (P / (n if n.endswith(".npz") else f"{n}_oof.npz")).exists()]
    if missing:
        print("WARNING missing OOF artifacts:", missing)

    a0 = np.load(P / "v2_0_baseline_anchor_oof.npz")
    oof_text, oof_id, fold_ids = a0["oof_text"], a0["oof_id"], a0["fold_ids"]

    m0 = json.loads((H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_0_baseline_anchor_metrics.json").read_text())
    m1 = json.loads((H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_1_text_ensemble_metrics.json").read_text())
    m2 = json.loads((H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_2_hand_features_metrics.json").read_text())
    m3 = json.loads((H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_3_per_class_alpha_metrics.json").read_text())

    # ---------------- reference rows ----------------
    add("ref_id_prior_only", "reference: ID-locality prior alone (k=20, 1/(d+1e-6))",
        y, oof_id, fold_ids,
        {"k": H.K_NEIGHBORS, "weights": "1/(distance+1e-6)", "pool": "fold-train ids only"})
    add("ref_text_lr_only", "reference: baseline LR text model alone (no ID prior)",
        y, oof_text, fold_ids,
        {"model": "LogisticRegression", **H.LR_KW,
         "features": "word(1-2) + char_wb(3-5) TF-IDF, 60k each, min_df=2"})
    add("ref_baseline_blend_alpha0.40",
        "LOCKED BASELINE (best-known-good): LR text + k=20 ID prior, blend alpha=0.40",
        y, H.blend(oof_text, oof_id, H.ALPHA_BASELINE), fold_ids,
        {"alpha": H.ALPHA_BASELINE, "k": H.K_NEIGHBORS, "model": "LogisticRegression",
         **H.LR_KW,
         "features": "word(1-2) + char_wb(3-5) TF-IDF, 60k each, min_df=2"},
        note="reproduced exactly by v2_0 (delta 0.000000)")

    # ---------------- EXP 1 ----------------
    e1 = np.load(P / "v2_1_text_ensemble_oof.npz")
    ens = e1["oof_ens"]
    t1 = m1["text_model"]
    base_cfg_1 = {
        "text_members": t1["members"], "combination": t1["combination"],
        "linear_svc": t1["linear_svc"], "calibration": t1["calibration"],
        "id_prior": {"k": H.K_NEIGHBORS, "weights": "1/(distance+1e-6)"},
    }
    add("v2_1_ens_equal_alpha0.40", f"{METRICS['v2_1_text_ensemble']} | alpha=0.40",
        y, H.blend(ens, oof_id, 0.40), fold_ids, {**base_cfg_1, "alpha": 0.40})
    add("v2_1_ens_equal_alpha_best0.45", f"{METRICS['v2_1_text_ensemble']} | alpha=0.45 "
                                         f"(re-gridded)", y,
        H.blend(ens, oof_id, m1["chosen_alpha"]), fold_ids,
        {**base_cfg_1, "alpha": m1["chosen_alpha"]},
        note="alpha re-gridded on pooled OOF; mild optimism")
    dw = m1["diagnostic_simplex_best"]
    add("v2_1_ens_simplex_tuned_alpha0.45",
        f"{METRICS['v2_1_text_ensemble']} | member weights tuned on OOF "
        f"(LR={dw['w_lr']}, SVC={dw['w_svc']}, CNB={dw['w_nb']})",
        y, H.blend(e1["oof_ens_w"], oof_id, m1["chosen_alpha"]), fold_ids,
        {**base_cfg_1, "alpha": m1["chosen_alpha"], "member_weights":
            [dw["w_lr"], dw["w_svc"], dw["w_nb"]]},
        note="OPTIMISTIC: member weights selected on the same pooled OOF")

    # ---------------- EXP 2 ----------------
    e2 = np.load(P / "v2_2_hand_features_oof.npz")
    f2 = m2["features"]
    for s in f2["block_scales_swept"]:
        arr = e2[f"oof_scale{s}"]
        add(f"v2_2_hand_scale{s}_alpha0.40",
            f"{METRICS['v2_2_hand_features']} | dense block scale={s}, alpha=0.40",
            y, H.blend(arr, oof_id, 0.40), fold_ids,
            {"dense_block_scale": s, "alpha": 0.40, "features": f2["names"],
             "scaler": f2["scaler"], "computed_on": f2["computed_on"],
             "model": "LogisticRegression", **H.LR_KW,
             "id_prior": {"k": H.K_NEIGHBORS}},
            note=("literal prompt specification" if s == 1.0 else "scale sweep"))
    s_best = max(f2["block_scales_swept"],
                 key=lambda s: m2["pooled_oof_macro_f1"][f"blend_scale{s}_0.40"])
    a_best = m2["chosen_alpha_by_scale"][str(s_best)]
    add(f"v2_2_hand_scale{s_best}_alpha{a_best}",
        f"{METRICS['v2_2_hand_features']} | best dense scale={s_best} + re-gridded alpha",
        y, H.blend(e2[f"oof_scale{s_best}"], oof_id, a_best), fold_ids,
        {"dense_block_scale": s_best, "alpha": a_best, "features": f2["names"],
         "scaler": f2["scaler"], "computed_on": f2["computed_on"],
         "model": "LogisticRegression", **H.LR_KW,
         "id_prior": {"k": H.K_NEIGHBORS}},
        note="OPTIMISTIC: scale and alpha both chosen on the same pooled OOF")

    # ---------------- EXP 3 ----------------
    def cross_fitted_proba(p_text, p_id, w_folds):
        proba = np.zeros((len(y), 3))
        for k_str, w in w_folds.items():
            k = int(k_str)
            _, va = H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
            proba[va] = H.blend_per_class(p_text[va], p_id[va], w)
        return proba

    r3 = m3["results"]
    for label, key in (("lr", "lr"), ("ens", "ens")):
        res = r3.get(label)
        if res is None:
            continue
        base_p = ens if label == "ens" else oof_text
        cfg3 = {"text_source": label, "id_prior": {"k": H.K_NEIGHBORS},
                "parametrisation": "P(c)=w_c*text_c+(1-w_c)*id_c, w in [0,1]^3",
                "optimiser": "scipy Nelder-Mead multi-start + local 0.01 grid"}
        head = res["per_class_tuned_headline"]
        add(f"v2_3_perclass_{label}_tuned_on_oof",
            f"{METRICS['v2_3_per_class_alpha']} | text={label} | w={np.round(head['w'],4).tolist()}",
            y, H.blend_per_class(base_p, oof_id, head["w"]), fold_ids,
            {**cfg3, "w": head["w"]},
            note="OPTIMISTIC: w tuned on the same pooled OOF that is scored")
        cf = res["per_class_cross_fitted"]
        add(f"v2_3_perclass_{label}_crossfitted",
            f"{METRICS['v2_3_per_class_alpha']} | text={label} | HONEST cross-fitted w",
            y, cross_fitted_proba(base_p, oof_id, cf["per_fold_w"]), fold_ids,
            {**cfg3, "w_per_fold": cf["per_fold_w"]},
            note="w tuned on 4 folds of OOF rows, scored on the held-out fold")

    # ---------------- EXP 4 ----------------
    m4 = json.loads((H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_4_meta_stacker_metrics.json").read_text())
    feat_names = m4["meta_learner"]["features"]
    from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
    from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
    raw = d["train"]["text"].to_numpy()
    Hraw = H.hand_feature_matrix(raw)
    mkw = dict(m4["meta_learner"])
    mkw.pop("features", None)
    mkw.pop("model", None)
    mkw.pop("scaler", None)
    mkw.pop("note", None)
    spec_blocks = {
        "text+id+hand": np.hstack([oof_text, oof_id, Hraw]),
        "text+id (no hand)": np.hstack([oof_text, oof_id]),
        "text+hand (no id)": np.hstack([oof_text, Hraw]),
        "id+hand (no text)": np.hstack([oof_id, Hraw]),
    }
    for res in m4["results"]:
        label = res["tag"].replace("[spec] ", "").replace(" (spec)", "")
        block = spec_blocks[label]
        pred = np.zeros(len(y), dtype=int)
        for k in range(H.N_SPLITS):
            tr, va = H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
            sc = StandardScaler().fit(block[tr])
            clf = LogisticRegression(**mkw).fit(sc.transform(block[tr]), y[tr])
            pred[va] = clf.predict(sc.transform(block[va]))
        add(f"v2_4_meta_{label.replace(' ', '_').replace('+', '')}",
            f"{METRICS['v2_4_meta_stacker']} | {label}",
            y, np.eye(3)[pred], fold_ids,
            {"meta_learner": "LogisticRegression", **mkw,
             "features": [n for n in feat_names][: block.shape[1]],
             "base": "grouped OOF text_probs + id_probs",
             "inner_cv": "same grouped 5-fold split (asserted identical)"},
            note="meta-learner cross-fitted: trained on 4 folds, scored on the 5th")
    if m4.get("strict_nested"):
        sp_path = H.CACHE_DIR / "strict_base_probs.npz"
        if sp_path.exists():
            s = np.load(sp_path)
            pred = np.zeros(len(y), dtype=int)
            block = np.hstack([s["oof_text"], s["oof_id"], Hraw])
            for k in range(H.N_SPLITS):
                tr, va = H.fold_split(fold_ids, k)
                sc = StandardScaler().fit(block[tr])
                clf = LogisticRegression(**mkw).fit(sc.transform(block[tr]), y[tr])
                pred[va] = clf.predict(sc.transform(block[va]))
            add("v2_4_meta_strict_text_id_hand",
                f"{METRICS['v2_4_meta_stacker']} | STRICT double-nested base features",
                y, np.eye(3)[pred], fold_ids,
                {"meta_learner": "LogisticRegression", **mkw,
                 "features": feat_names,
                 "base": "double-nested: meta-train base models never saw the "
                         "meta-eval fold either"},
                note="base models here see 3/5 of the data, so this row is NOT "
                     "directly comparable to 0.639579; the in-protocol reference "
                     f"(plain blend on the same strict base) is "
                     f"{m4['strict_nested']['reference_blend_0.40_on_strict_base']}")

    # ---------------- EXP 5 (if present) ----------------
    m5_path = H.REPORTS_DIR / "v2_5_combined_metrics.json"
    if m5_path.exists():
        m5 = json.loads(m5_path.read_text())
        e5 = np.load(P / "v2_5_combined_oof.npz")
        add("v2_5_combined_best",
            f"{METRICS['v2_5_combined']} | gate={m5['gate']['any_single_experiment_beat_baseline']}",
            y, e5["oof_best"], fold_ids, m5["best_config"],
            note=m5["gate"]["note"])

    # ---------------- print ----------------
    tbl = H.read_comparison()
    cols = ["experiment", "oof_macro_f1", "delta_vs_baseline", "per_fold_std"]
    winners = tbl["delta_vs_baseline"].astype(float) > 0
    print("\n=== reports/v2_comparison_table.csv (pooled leak-free OOF Macro F1) ===")
    print(tbl[cols].to_string(index=False))
    print(f"\nbaseline = {H.BASELINE_OOF_F1:.6f} | rows with delta > 0: "
          f"{tbl.loc[winners, 'experiment'].tolist()}")
    H.check_protected()
    print(f"\ntable -> {H.COMPARISON_CSV.relative_to(H.ROOT).as_posix()}  "
          f"({len(tbl)} rows)")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


============================================================
FILE: .\src\experiment_v3_1_multiscale_id.py
============================================================

"""
Experiment v3.1: Multi-scale and directional ID structure exploration.
Authoritative evaluation on StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42).
Checks ID autocorrelation, directional priors, multi-scale k, learned LightGBM ID model,
and brute-force leak proof assertions.
"""

import os, sys, json, time, hashlib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.metrics import f1_score, accuracy_score

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
import experiment_v2_harness as h

SEED = 42
np.random.seed(SEED)

def compute_prior_oof(train, folds, k=20, direction='symmetric', kernel='inverse'):
    P_all = np.zeros((len(train), 3), dtype=np.float64)
    for f in range(5):
        val_idx = np.where(folds == f)[0]
        tr_idx = np.where(folds != f)[0]
        q_ids = train.loc[val_idx, 'id'].values
        tr_ids = train.loc[tr_idx, 'id'].values
        tr_y = train.loc[tr_idx, 'y'].values
        
        order = np.argsort(tr_ids)
        s_ids = tr_ids[order]
        s_y = tr_y[order]
        n = len(s_ids)
        pos = np.searchsorted(s_ids, q_ids, side='left')
        
        if direction == 'symmetric':
            cand = pos[:, None] + np.arange(-k, k + 1)[None, :]
        elif direction == 'left_only':
            cand = pos[:, None] + np.arange(-k, 0)[None, :]
        elif direction == 'right_only':
            cand = pos[:, None] + np.arange(0, k)[None, :]
            
        valid = (cand >= 0) & (cand < n)
        cc = np.clip(cand, 0, n - 1)
        d = np.abs(s_ids[cc] - q_ids[:, None]).astype(np.float64)
        d = np.where(valid & (s_ids[cc] != q_ids[:, None]), d, np.inf)
        
        kk = min(k, cand.shape[1])
        sel = np.argsort(d, axis=1, kind='stable')[:, :kk]
        dsel = np.take_along_axis(d, sel, axis=1)
        ysel = s_y[cc[np.arange(len(q_ids))[:, None], sel]]
        
        good = np.isfinite(dsel)
        if kernel == 'inverse':
            w = np.where(good, 1.0 / (np.where(good, dsel, 0.0) + 1e-6), 0.0)
        elif kernel == 'inverse_sqrt':
            w = np.where(good, 1.0 / np.sqrt(np.where(good, dsel, 0.0) + 1.0), 0.0)
        elif kernel == 'uniform':
            w = np.where(good, 1.0, 0.0)
            
        P = np.stack([(w * (ysel == c)).sum(axis=1) for c in range(3)], axis=1)
        tot = P.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        P = P / np.where(tot > 0, tot, 1.0)
        P[tot.ravel() <= 0] = 1.0 / 3
        P_all[val_idx] = P
    return P_all
def main():
    print("=" * 78)
    print("EXPERIMENT v3.1 -- Multi-scale Directional ID Structure & Models")
    print("=" * 78)

    os.makedirs('reports/v3_id_structure', exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs('reports', exist_ok=True)

    data = h.load_data()
    train = data['train']
    folds = np.load('cache/v2/fold_ids.npy')
    train['fold'] = folds
    ys = train['y'].values

    base_anchor = np.load('predictions/v2/v2_0_baseline_anchor_oof.npz')
    oof_text = base_anchor['oof_text']
    oof_id_k20 = base_anchor['oof_id']

    # 1. Multi-scale ID Prior Sweep
    print("Evaluating multi-scale k priors...")
    p_k30 = compute_prior_oof(train, folds, k=30)
    p_k20 = oof_id_k20

    blend_k20 = 0.4 * oof_text + 0.6 * p_k20
    score_k20 = f1_score(ys, blend_k20.argmax(axis=1), average='macro')
    fold_scores_k20 = [f1_score(ys[folds == f], blend_k20[folds == f].argmax(axis=1), average='macro') for f in range(5)]

    blend_k30 = 0.4 * oof_text + 0.6 * p_k30
    score_k30 = f1_score(ys, blend_k30.argmax(axis=1), average='macro')
    fold_scores_k30 = [f1_score(ys[folds == f], blend_k30[folds == f].argmax(axis=1), average='macro') for f in range(5)]

    print(f"k=20 Blend F1 (Baseline): {score_k20:.6f} | per-fold std: {np.std(fold_scores_k20):.4f}")
    print(f"k=30 Blend F1 (Diagnostic): {score_k30:.6f} (delta: {score_k30 - 0.639579:+.6f}) | per-fold std: {np.std(fold_scores_k30):.4f}")

    # Honest cross-fitting for k=30
    oof_honest_k30 = np.zeros(len(train), dtype=np.int64)
    for f in range(5):
        tr_m = (folds != f)
        val_m = (folds == f)
        b_a = 0.40
        b_s = -1
        for a in np.linspace(0.30, 0.50, 21):
            s = f1_score(ys[tr_m], (a * oof_text[tr_m] + (1 - a) * p_k30[tr_m]).argmax(axis=1), average='macro')
            if s > b_s:
                b_s = s
                b_a = a
        oof_honest_k30[val_m] = (b_a * oof_text[val_m] + (1 - b_a) * p_k30[val_m]).argmax(axis=1)

    f1_k30_honest = f1_score(ys, oof_honest_k30, average='macro')
    f_honest_k30 = [f1_score(ys[folds == f], oof_honest_k30[folds == f], average='macro') for f in range(5)]
    print(f"k=30 Blend F1 (HONEST cross-fitted): {f1_k30_honest:.6f} (delta: {f1_k30_honest - 0.639579:+.6f})")

    delta = f1_k30_honest - 0.639579
    status = "REJECTED" if delta < 0 else "MARGINAL"
    print(f"Decision Gate for Experiment 1: {status} (honest delta: {delta:+.6f})")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


============================================================
FILE: .\src\experiment_v3_3_frozen_embeddings.py
============================================================

"""
Experiment v3.3 -- CPU-feasible FROZEN semantic embeddings (no fine-tuning).

Model: intfloat/multilingual-e5-small (12-layer, 384-dim, 100 languages incl. Bangla).
Frozen inference only, CPU, 8 threads. NO fine-tuning. NO external LABELED data.

Uses the EXACT canonical grouped 5-fold split already validated in this project:
    StratifiedGroupKFold(5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)
    group = clean_text(text).lower().strip()
Fold identity is re-verified against cache/v2/fold_ids.npy before use.

Outputs:
    cache/v3/emb_e5_small_train.npy     (47817, 384) float32 normalized
    cache/v3/emb_e5_small_test.npy      (11955, 384) float32 normalized
    reports/v3_configs/v3_frozen_emb_*.json
    predictions/v3_candidates/CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_*_test_probs.npy  (.npy only)
"""

import os
import sys
import gc
import json
import time
import hashlib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import experiment_v2_harness as h

SEED = 42
MODEL_NAME = "intfloat/multilingual-e5-small"
EMB_DIM = 384
BASELINE = 0.639579
CACHE_DIR = "cache/v3"
TEST_CACHE = os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, "emb_e5_small_test.npy")
TRAIN_CACHE = os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, "emb_e5_small_train.npy")

torch.set_num_threads(8)
np.random.seed(SEED)


def protected_check(stage):
    mf = "reports/v3_protected_manifest.json"
    if not os.path.exists(mf):
        return
    with open(mf) as f:
        manifest = json.load(f)
    for path, info in manifest.items():
        assert os.path.exists(path), f"[{stage}] MISSING protected file {path}"
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            cur = hashlib.sha256(fh.read()).hexdigest()
        assert cur == info["sha256"], f"[{stage}] PROTECTED FILE MODIFIED: {path}"
    print(f"  protected-file check @ {stage}: OK (5/5 unchanged)")


def encode_all():
    os.makedirs(CACHE_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

    tr = pd.read_csv("train.csv")
    if "clean" not in tr.columns:
        tr["clean"] = tr["text"].map(h.clean_text)
    te = pd.read_csv("test.csv")

    model = SentenceTransformer(MODEL_NAME, device="cpu")

    if os.path.exists(TRAIN_CACHE) and os.path.exists(TEST_CACHE):
        print("embeddings already cached -> load")
        return np.load(TRAIN_CACHE), np.load(TEST_CACHE), tr, te

    t0 = time.time()
    print("encoding TRAIN texts ...", flush=True)
    tr_emb = model.encode(
        tr["text"].astype(str).tolist(),
        batch_size=64,
        show_progress_bar=True,
        normalize_embeddings=True,
        convert_to_numpy=True,
    ).astype(np.float32)
    print(f"  train embeddings {tr_emb.shape} in {round(time.time() - t0, 1)}s", flush=True)

    t1 = time.time()
    print("encoding TEST texts ...", flush=True)
    te_emb = model.encode(
        te["text"].astype(str).tolist(),
        batch_size=64,
        show_progress_bar=True,
        normalize_embeddings=True,
        convert_to_numpy=True,
    ).astype(np.float32)
    print(f"  test embeddings {te_emb.shape} in {round(time.time() - t1, 1)}s", flush=True)

    np.save(TRAIN_CACHE, tr_emb)
    np.save(TEST_CACHE, te_emb)
    del model
    gc.collect()
    return tr_emb, te_emb, tr, te


def verify_folds(train):
    folds = np.load("cache/v2/fold_ids.npy")
    rebuilt = h.build_folds(train["clean"], train["y"], verbose=False)
    assert np.array_equal(folds, rebuilt), "canonical fold identity drift!"
    assert len(folds) == 47817
    assert set(np.unique(folds).tolist()) == {0, 1, 2, 3, 4}
    sha = hashlib.sha256(folds.tobytes()).hexdigest()
    print(f"  canonical folds verified | SHA256 = {sha}")
    return folds, sha
def main():
    t_start = time.time()
    print("=" * 78)
    print("EXPERIMENT v3.3 -- Frozen multilingual-e5-small embeddings (CPU only)")
    print("=" * 78)
    os.makedirs("reports/v3_configs", exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs("predictions/v3_candidates", exist_ok=True)

    protected_check("startup")

    tr_emb, te_emb, tr, te = encode_all()
    folds, fold_sha = verify_folds(tr)

    from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
    from sklearn.metrics import f1_score

    y = tr["y"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
    te_ids = te["id"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int64)

    assert np.isfinite(tr_emb).all(), "non-finite train embeddings"
    assert np.isfinite(te_emb).all(), "non-finite test embeddings"

    # ---------- Stage 1: frozen-embedding LR standalone, canonical folds ----------
    oof_emb = np.zeros((len(tr), 3), dtype=np.float64)
    for f in range(5):
        val = folds == f
        trn = ~val
        clf = LogisticRegression(C=4.0, class_weight="balanced", max_iter=2000,
                                 solver="lbfgs", random_state=SEED)
        clf.fit(tr_emb[trn], y[trn])
        oof_emb[val] = clf.predict_proba(tr_emb[val])
        print(f"  fold {f}: embedding-LR val macro F1 = "
              f"{f1_score(y[val], oof_emb[val].argmax(1), average='macro'):.6f}", flush=True)

    emb_standalone = f1_score(y, oof_emb.argmax(1), average="macro")
    emb_per_class = f1_score(y, oof_emb.argmax(1), average=None)
    emb_fold_scores = [f1_score(y[folds == f], oof_emb[folds == f].argmax(1), average="macro")
                       for f in range(5)]
    print(f"\nFrozen-embedding LR standalone OOF Macro F1 = {emb_standalone:.6f}")
