    print(f"  class0={emb_per_class[0]:.6f} class1={emb_per_class[1]:.6f} class2={emb_per_class[2]:.6f}")
    print(f"  fold scores = {[round(s, 6) for s in emb_fold_scores]} std={np.std(emb_fold_scores):.6f}")

    # ---------- Stage 2: test probabilities for candidate artifact (.npy only) ----------
    clf_full = LogisticRegression(C=4.0, class_weight="balanced", max_iter=2000,
                                  solver="lbfgs", random_state=SEED)
    clf_full.fit(tr_emb, y)
    te_emb_prob = clf_full.predict_proba(te_emb)
    te_cand_path = "predictions/v3_candidates/CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_v3_frozen_emb_test_probs.npy"
    np.save(te_cand_path, te_emb_prob)
    assert np.isfinite(te_emb_prob).all()
    assert np.allclose(te_emb_prob.sum(axis=1), 1.0, atol=1e-6)
    # ---------- Stage 3: 3-way honest blend with existing signals ----------
    base = np.load("predictions/v2/v2_0_baseline_anchor_oof.npz")
    oof_text = base["oof_text"]
    oof_id = base["oof_id"]
    all3 = np.load("cache/t0/oof_all3.npz")
    oof_text_ens = 0.5 * oof_text + 0.5 * all3["oof_text"]

    def norm(P):
        return P / P.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)

    grid = np.arange(0.0, 1.0001, 0.1)
    diag = []
    for w_t in grid:
        for w_i in grid:
            w_e = 1.0 - w_t - w_i
            if w_e < -1e-9:
                continue
            P = norm(w_t * oof_text_ens + w_i * oof_id + w_e * oof_emb)
            diag.append((f1_score(y, P.argmax(1), average="macro"), round(float(w_t), 2),
                         round(float(w_i), 2), round(float(w_e), 2)))
    diag.sort(key=lambda r: r[0], reverse=True)
    print("\nTop-10 same-OOF diagnostic simplex points (NOT eligible):")
    for row in diag[:10]:
        print(f"   macro_f1={row[0]:.6f}  w_text={row[1]}  w_id={row[2]}  w_emb={row[3]}")

    # honest cross-fitted simplex: weights chosen on the other 4 folds only
    oof_final = np.zeros((len(tr), 3), dtype=np.float64)
    honest_pred = np.zeros(len(tr), dtype=np.int64)
    chosen = {}
    for f in range(5):
        val = folds == f
        trn = ~val
        best = (-1.0, None)
        for w_t in grid:
            for w_i in grid:
                w_e = 1.0 - w_t - w_i
                if w_e < -1e-9:
                    continue
                P = norm(w_t * oof_text_ens[trn] + w_i * oof_id[trn] + w_e * oof_emb[trn])
                s = f1_score(y[trn], P.argmax(1), average="macro")
                if s > best[0]:
                    best = (s, (float(w_t), float(w_i), float(w_e)))
        w_t, w_i, w_e = best[1]
        chosen[str(f)] = {"w_text": w_t, "w_id": w_i, "w_emb": w_e, "train_f1": round(best[0], 6)}
        P = norm(w_t * oof_text_ens[val] + w_i * oof_id[val] + w_e * oof_emb[val])
        oof_final[val] = P
        honest_pred[val] = P.argmax(1)
        print(f"  fold {f}: chose w_text={w_t} w_id={w_i} w_emb={w_e} "
              f"(train {best[0]:.6f}) -> val {f1_score(y[val], honest_pred[val], average='macro'):.6f}",
              flush=True)

    honest_f1 = f1_score(y, honest_pred, average="macro")
    honest_per_class = f1_score(y, honest_pred, average=None)
    honest_fold_scores = [f1_score(y[folds == f], honest_pred[folds == f], average="macro") for f in range(5)]
    print(f"\nHONEST 3-way cross-fitted Macro F1 = {honest_f1:.6f} (delta {honest_f1 - BASELINE:+.6f})")
    print(f"  class0={honest_per_class[0]:.6f} class1={honest_per_class[1]:.6f} class2={honest_per_class[2]:.6f}")
    print(f"  fold std = {np.std(honest_fold_scores):.6f}")

    base_pred = norm(0.4 * oof_text_ens + 0.6 * oof_id).argmax(1)
    emb_pred = oof_emb.argmax(1)
    dis = float((base_pred != emb_pred).mean())
    b_err = base_pred != y
    e_err = emb_pred != y
    fixed = int(((~b_err) & (emb_pred == y)).sum())
    damaged = int((b_err & (emb_pred != y)).sum())
    payload = {
        "experiment_id": "v3_3_frozen_e5_small",
        "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "description": "Frozen intfloat/multilingual-e5-small (CPU, no fine-tune) + LR; standalone and honest 3-way blend",
        "model": MODEL_NAME,
        "mode": "FROZEN_INFERENCE_ONLY_NO_FINETUNING",
        "embedding_dim": EMB_DIM,
        "device": "cpu",
        "validation": {"method": "StratifiedGroupKFold", "n_splits": 5, "shuffle": True,
                       "random_state": SEED, "group_key": "clean_text(text).lower().strip()",
                       "metric": "pooled OOF Macro F1"},
        "fold_sha256": fold_sha,
        "standalone": {
            "oof_macro_f1": round(float(emb_standalone), 6),
            "per_class_f1": [round(float(v), 6) for v in emb_per_class],
            "fold_scores": [round(float(s), 6) for s in emb_fold_scores],
            "fold_std": round(float(np.std(emb_fold_scores)), 6),
        },
        "blend_3way": {
            "same_oof_diagnostic_top10": [
                {"macro_f1": round(float(r[0]), 6), "w_text": r[1], "w_id": r[2], "w_emb": r[3]}
                for r in diag[:10]
            ],
            "honest_cross_fitted_macro_f1": round(float(honest_f1), 6),
            "honest_delta_vs_baseline": round(float(honest_f1 - BASELINE), 6),
            "honest_per_class_f1": [round(float(v), 6) for v in honest_per_class],
            "honest_fold_scores": [round(float(s), 6) for s in honest_fold_scores],
            "honest_fold_std": round(float(np.std(honest_fold_scores)), 6),
            "per_fold_chosen_weights": chosen,
        },
        "complementarity": {
            "disagreement_rate_vs_locked_blend": round(dis, 6),
            "emb_corrected_rows": fixed,
            "emb_damaged_rows": damaged,
        },
        "artifacts": {
            "train_embeddings": TRAIN_CACHE,
            "test_embeddings": TEST_CACHE,
            "oof_probs": "predictions/v3_candidates/CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_v3_3way_oof_probs.npy",
            "test_probs": te_cand_path,
        },
        "submission_written": False,
        "runtime_seconds": round(time.time() - t_start, 1),
    }
    with open("reports/v3_configs/v3_frozen_emb_metrics.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump(payload, f, indent=2)
    print("\n-> reports/v3_configs/v3_frozen_emb_metrics.json | NO submission written")

    protected_check("end")
    print(f"runtime {payload['runtime_seconds']}s")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

    print(f"\ncomplementarity: disagreement={dis:.4f} | emb fixes {fixed} rows, damages {damaged} rows")
    for c in (1, 2):
        m = (y == c)
        print(f"  class {c}: base acc={float((base_pred[m] == y[m]).mean()):.4f} "
              f"emb acc={float((emb_pred[m] == y[m]).mean()):.4f}")

    np.save("predictions/v3_candidates/CANDIDATE_NOT_FOR_SUBMISSION_v3_3way_oof_probs.npy", oof_final)

    print(f"\ncandidate test probs saved: {te_cand_path} (no CSV written)")

"""
src/make_repro_notebook.py -- builds the self-contained competition notebook

Output: uap_submission/reproducible/submission_reproducible_file_1.ipynb

The notebook is fully self-contained (no imports from src/, no downloads, no
hardcoded paths): it reads train.csv / test.csv / sample_submission.csv from its
own directory (relative paths only) and writes ./submission/submission_1.csv.

Run:  python src/make_repro_notebook.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import nbformat as nbf

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
OUT_DIR = ROOT / "uap_submission" / "reproducible"
OUT_IPYNB = OUT_DIR / "submission_reproducible_file_1.ipynb"

# Numbers documented from PART 1 (reports/revalidation_grouped_metrics.json).
PART1_ALPHA = 0.40
PART1_OOF = 0.639579
PART1_FOLDS = [0.642898, 0.632324, 0.641935, 0.639295, 0.641384]
PART1_TEXT = 0.512943
PART1_ID = 0.614135
PART1_PER_CLASS = (0.757270, 0.620188, 0.541279)

cells: list = []


def md(src: str) -> None:
    cells.append(nbf.v4.new_markdown_cell(src.strip("\n")))


def code(src: str) -> None:
    cells.append(nbf.v4.new_code_cell(src.strip("\n")))


MD_INTRO = f"""
# HerWILL X UAP Datathon 2026 -- reproducible submission #1

**Model:** word + character TF-IDF with balanced Logistic Regression (C = 4.0),
blended with an **ID-locality prior** at **alpha = {PART1_ALPHA:.2f}**, plus a
duplicate-text override rule.

This notebook is **fully self-contained**: no Google Drive mount, no hardcoded
personal paths, no external downloads, no pretrained weights, no network access.

**How to run it**

1. Place `train.csv`, `test.csv` and `sample_submission.csv` in the *same
   directory as this notebook*.
2. Restart the kernel and run all cells top to bottom (`Restart & Run All`).
3. The notebook writes `./submission/submission_1.csv` (relative path).

**Seeds:** `SEED = 42` is applied to `random`, `numpy`, scikit-learn's estimators
and the cross-validation splitter, so repeated runs are reproducible.

### Data overview

| file | rows | columns | notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `train.csv` | 47,817 | `id`, `text`, `y` | 0 = Explicitly Toxic/Hateful, 1 = Subtly Toxic, 2 = Non-toxic/Neutral |
| `test.csv` | 11,955 | `id`, `text` | no labels; ids continue the train id range |
| `sample_submission.csv` | 11,955 | `id`, `y_pred` | defines the required row order and schema |

Train + test ids together form the **contiguous range 0..59771 with zero
overlap** (verified during re-validation), and the text is bilingual (Bangla,
English, Banglish/mixed) with emoji, punctuation and hashtags preserved.

Class balance in `train.csv`: 0 -> 23,756 (49.68%), 1 -> 18,163 (37.98%),
2 -> 5,898 (12.33%). Primary metric: **Macro F1**.
"""

MD_ARCH = f"""
### Model architecture

Two TF-IDF views of the **cleaned** text are hstacked into one sparse matrix:

| block | settings |
| --- | --- |
| word TF-IDF | `analyzer="word"`, `ngram_range=(1, 2)`, `min_df=2`, `max_features=60000`, `sublinear_tf=True` |
| char TF-IDF | `analyzer="char_wb"`, `ngram_range=(3, 5)`, `min_df=2`, `max_features=60000`, `sublinear_tf=True` |

Cleaning is deliberately minimal - only URL and mention masking: the literal
`URL -> " URL "` and `@mention -> " USER "`, then whitespace collapsed. Nothing
else is changed: no lowercasing, no stemming, and Bangla script / emoji /
hashtags / repeated characters / punctuation are preserved.

Classifier: `LogisticRegression(solver="lbfgs", C=4.0, class_weight="balanced",
max_iter=2000, random_state=42)` - native multinomial `lbfgs` with balanced
class weights handles the 3-class problem and the class imbalance directly.
Per-class probabilities come from `predict_proba`.

### Leakage control

The TF-IDF vocabulary is fitted **inside each CV fold on that fold's training
split only**, then once more on the full training set for the final model.
`dtype=float32` is used for the sparse matrices (memory only; it does not change
the model).
"""


MD_PRIOR = """
### ID-locality prior (transductive, competition-specific -- not for production)

**Formula.** For a query id `q` and a pool of labelled training ids `T` with
labels `y`:

1. take the `k = 20` nearest ids of `q` by `|t - q|` (ties broken by the smaller
   id, so the rule is deterministic),
2. weight each neighbour by `w = 1 / (distance + 1e-6)`,
3. sum the weights per class and normalise:

```
P(class = c | q) = sum_{j in kNN(q)} w_j * 1[y_j = c]  /  sum_{j in kNN(q)} w_j
```

Fewer than `k` available neighbours -> use whatever exists; a query with no
usable neighbour falls back to the uniform `1/3` prior.

**Why it works here.** `train.csv` and `test.csv` ids are contiguous and
non-overlapping, so nearby ids come from the same scrape order (same threads /
bursts) and labels are *locally correlated along the id axis*. That makes the id
axis a predictive signal **for this file only**.

**Why it is NOT a production feature.** It is *transductive*: it exploits how the
competition files were interleaved. On new data, or if ids were randomised, the
signal disappears. It must never be reused as a general-purpose feature outside
this competition.

**Leak-safety.** The prior only ever receives training ids and training labels,
so validation/test labels cannot enter the pool; the re-validation asserted
`val_ids (intersection) pool = empty` for all five folds.
"""

MD_CV = """
### Validation protocol (Part 1 re-validation)

`StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)` where the
**group is the cleaned + lowercased text**. Every duplicate text therefore stays
in a single fold, removing the duplicate-text train/val leakage that plain
`StratifiedKFold` allowed (previously disclosed as an optimism source).

Part-1 independent re-validation (everything recomputed from scratch):

| metric | value |
| --- | --- |
| text-only OOF Macro F1 | **{text}** |
| id-prior-only OOF Macro F1 | **{idv}** |
| blend @ alpha = {alpha:.2f} OOF Macro F1 | **{oof}** |
| per-fold blend Macro F1 | {folds} |
| per-class F1 (blend) | {pc} |
| groups / duplicate groups / groups crossing folds | 47,157 / 480 / 0 |

The blend sits slightly below the earlier plain-stratified number (0.642185),
which is expected with leakage-free folds and was **not** chased: alpha was
re-searched on the new folds and **{alpha:.2f} remained the argmax**, so 0.40 is
the final chosen alpha.
""".format(text=f"{PART1_TEXT:.6f}", idv=f"{PART1_ID:.6f}", alpha=PART1_ALPHA,
           oof=f"{PART1_OOF:.6f}",
           folds=", ".join(f"{f:.6f}" for f in PART1_FOLDS),
           pc=" / ".join(f"{v:.6f}" for v in PART1_PER_CLASS))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Code cells (raw strings: they contain regexes)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
CODE_ENV = r"""
import random, re, sys, time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import scipy.sparse as sp
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, confusion_matrix, f1_score
from sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedGroupKFold

SEED = 42
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
print("python:", sys.version.split()[0], "| numpy:", np.__version__,
      "| pandas:", pd.__version__)
print("SEED =", SEED, "-> random.seed, np.random.seed, sklearn random_state=SEED")
"""

CODE_MODEL = r"""
URL_RE = re.compile(r"(?:https?://|www\.)\S+", flags=re.IGNORECASE)
MENTION_RE = re.compile(r"@\w+")
WS_RE = re.compile(r"\s+", flags=re.UNICODE)


def clean_text(text):
    # URL -> ' URL ', @mention -> ' USER ', collapse whitespace. Nothing else.
    s = "" if text is None else str(text)
    s = URL_RE.sub(" URL ", s)
    s = MENTION_RE.sub(" USER ", s)
    return WS_RE.sub(" ", s).strip()


WORD_KW = dict(analyzer="word", ngram_range=(1, 2), min_df=2, max_features=60000,
               sublinear_tf=True, dtype=np.float32)
CHAR_KW = dict(analyzer="char_wb", ngram_range=(3, 5), min_df=2, max_features=60000,
               sublinear_tf=True, dtype=np.float32)
LR_KW = dict(solver="lbfgs", C=4.0, class_weight="balanced", max_iter=2000,
             random_state=SEED)
ALPHA_GRID = [round(0.05 * i, 2) for i in range(21)]
K_NEIGHBORS, ID_EPS = 20, 1e-6
CLASSES = (0, 1, 2)


def build_matrix(word, char, texts):
    return sp.hstack([word.transform(texts), char.transform(texts)],
                     format="csr").astype(np.float32)


def fit_text_model(texts, y, tag="model"):
    t0 = time.time()
    word = TfidfVectorizer(**WORD_KW)
    word.fit(texts)
    char = TfidfVectorizer(**CHAR_KW)
    char.fit(texts)
    X = build_matrix(word, char, texts)
    clf = LogisticRegression(**LR_KW)
    clf.fit(X, y)
    print(f"{tag}: X={X.shape} nnz={X.nnz:,} word={len(word.vocabulary_):,} "
          f"char={len(char.vocabulary_):,} fit={time.time() - t0:.1f}s")
    return word, char, clf


def blend(p_text, p_id, alpha):
    p = alpha * np.asarray(p_text) + (1.0 - alpha) * np.asarray(p_id)
    return p / p.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)


def macro_f1(y_true, proba):
    return float(f1_score(y_true, np.asarray(proba).argmax(axis=1), average="macro",
                          labels=list(CLASSES), zero_division=0))


def unanimous_lookup(texts, labels):
    # cleaned text -> label for groups where EVERY occurrence agrees (train only).
    df = pd.DataFrame({"t": pd.Series(list(texts), dtype="object"),
                       "y": np.asarray(labels)})
    g = df.groupby("t")["y"].agg(["nunique", "first"])
    uni = g[g["nunique"] == 1]
    lookup = {str(t): int(v) for t, v in uni["first"].items()}
    n_conflicting = int((g["nunique"] > 1).sum())
    return lookup, n_conflicting


def apply_override(texts, pred, lookup):
    out = np.asarray(pred).copy()
    hits = 0
    for i, t in enumerate(texts):
        v = lookup.get(str(t))
        if v is not None:
            out[i] = v
            hits += 1
    return out, hits
"""

CODE_DATA = r"""
def find_data(name):
    # Locate a competition CSV using RELATIVE paths only (no hardcoded drives).
    for base in (Path.cwd(), Path.cwd().parent):
        p = base / name
        if p.exists():
            return p
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"{name} not found - place train.csv / test.csv / sample_submission.csv "
        f"next to this notebook (current directory: {Path.cwd()})")


TRAIN_CSV = find_data("train.csv")
TEST_CSV = find_data("test.csv")
SAMPLE_CSV = find_data("sample_submission.csv")
print("data directory:", TRAIN_CSV.parent)

train = pd.read_csv(TRAIN_CSV, encoding="utf-8")
test = pd.read_csv(TEST_CSV, encoding="utf-8")
sample = pd.read_csv(SAMPLE_CSV, encoding="utf-8")
assert list(train.columns) == ["id", "text", "y"], list(train.columns)
assert list(test.columns) == ["id", "text"], list(test.columns)
assert list(sample.columns) == ["id", "y_pred"], list(sample.columns)

for df in (train, test):
    df["text"] = df["text"].astype("string").fillna("").astype(str)
train["clean"] = train["text"].map(clean_text)
test["clean"] = test["text"].map(clean_text)

y = train["y"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
ids = train["id"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int64)
test_ids = test["id"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int64)
clean_texts = train["clean"].to_numpy()     # OBJECT array (never np.asarray(list))
test_clean = test["clean"].to_numpy()
assert set(map(int, np.unique(y))) <= {0, 1, 2}
assert len(set(ids) & set(test_ids)) == 0, "train/test ids must not overlap"
assert len(sample) == len(test), "sample_submission must match test row count"
print("train", train.shape, "| test", test.shape, "| sample", sample.shape)
print("class counts:", {int(c): int((y == c).sum()) for c in CLASSES})
"""

CODE_PRIOR = r"""
def id_prior(query_ids, train_ids, train_labels, k=K_NEIGHBORS, eps=ID_EPS,
             n_classes=3, tag="prior"):
    # k-nearest-id label prior (vectorised). Only TRAIN ids/labels are passed in,
    # so a validation/test row's own label can never influence its own prediction.
    q = np.asarray(query_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    t = np.asarray(train_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    yy = np.asarray(train_labels, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    assert t.size == yy.size and t.size == np.unique(t).size
    order = np.argsort(t, kind="stable")           # ascending id -> deterministic ties
    s_ids, s_y = t[order], yy[order]
    n, kk = len(s_ids), min(k, len(s_ids))

    pos = np.searchsorted(s_ids, q, side="left")
    cand = pos[:, None] + np.arange(-k, k + 1)[None, :]      # k left + k right candidates
    valid = (cand >= 0) & (cand < n)
    cc = np.clip(cand, 0, n - 1)
    d = np.abs(s_ids[cc] - q[:, None]).astype(np.float64)
    d = np.where(valid & (s_ids[cc] != q[:, None]), d, np.inf)   # no self-match
    sel = np.argsort(d, axis=1, kind="stable")[:, :kk]           # exact k nearest
    dsel = np.take_along_axis(d, sel, axis=1)
    ysel = s_y[cc[np.arange(len(q))[:, None], sel]]

    good = np.isfinite(dsel)
    w = np.where(good, 1.0 / (np.where(good, dsel, 0.0) + eps), 0.0)
    P = np.stack([(w * (ysel == c)).sum(axis=1) for c in range(n_classes)], axis=1)
    tot = P.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    P = P / np.where(tot > 0, tot, 1.0)
    P[tot.ravel() <= 0] = 1.0 / n_classes
    overlap = int(np.isin(q, s_ids).sum())
    if overlap:
        print(f"WARNING {tag}: {overlap} query ids also in pool -> self-matches excluded")
    print(f"{tag}: queries={len(q)} pool={n} k={k} pool_disjoint_from_queries={overlap == 0}")
    assert overlap == 0, "leak: query ids present in the prior pool"
    return P
"""

CODE_CV = r"""
# ---- duplicate-safe folds: group = cleaned + lowercased text ----
keys = pd.Index([str(t).lower().strip() for t in clean_texts], dtype="object")
groups = pd.factorize(keys, sort=False)[0]
dup_groups = int((pd.Series(groups).value_counts() > 1).sum())
print("groups:", len(np.unique(groups)), "| duplicate groups:", dup_groups)

sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)
fold_ids = np.full(len(y), -1, dtype=int)
for k, (_tr, va) in enumerate(sgkf.split(np.zeros(len(y)), y, groups)):
    fold_ids[va] = k
crossing = pd.DataFrame({"g": groups, "f": fold_ids}).groupby("g")["f"].nunique()
print("groups spanning >1 fold:", int((crossing > 1).sum()))
assert int((crossing > 1).sum()) == 0, "duplicate text leaked across folds"

oof_text = np.zeros((len(y), 3))
oof_id = np.zeros((len(y), 3))
oof_override = np.zeros(len(y), dtype=int)
fold_rows = []
for k in range(5):
    va = np.flatnonzero(fold_ids == k)
    tr = np.flatnonzero(fold_ids != k)
    assert int(np.intersect1d(ids[va], ids[tr]).size) == 0  # prior pool vs val ids

    word, char, clf = fit_text_model(clean_texts[tr], y[tr], tag=f"fold{k}")
    p_text = clf.predict_proba(build_matrix(word, char, clean_texts[va]))
    p_id = id_prior(ids[va], ids[tr], y[tr], tag=f"fold{k} prior")
    p_blend = blend(p_text, p_id, 0.40)

    # leak-free override: lookup built ONLY from this fold's training split
    lookup, n_conflict = unanimous_lookup(clean_texts[tr], y[tr])
    assert set(lookup).issubset(set(clean_texts[tr].tolist())), "override leak"
    ov, hits = apply_override(clean_texts[va], p_blend.argmax(1), lookup)

    m_t, m_i, m_b = (macro_f1(y[va], p_text), macro_f1(y[va], p_id),
                     macro_f1(y[va], p_blend))
    m_o = float(f1_score(y[va], ov, average="macro", labels=list(CLASSES),
                         zero_division=0))
    oof_text[va] = p_text
    oof_id[va] = p_id
    oof_override[va] = ov
    fold_rows.append({"fold": k, "n_val": len(va), "text": round(m_t, 6),
                      "id_prior": round(m_i, 6), "blend_0.40": round(m_b, 6),
                      "blend_override": round(m_o, 6), "override_rows": hits})
    print(f"fold{k}: text={m_t:.6f} id={m_i:.6f} blend@0.40={m_b:.6f} | "
          f"override_rows={hits} ({m_b:.6f} -> {m_o:.6f})")

print(pd.DataFrame(fold_rows).to_string(index=False))
print(f"pooled OOF: text={macro_f1(y, oof_text):.6f} "
      f"id={macro_f1(y, oof_id):.6f} "
      f"blend@0.40={macro_f1(y, blend(oof_text, oof_id, 0.40)):.6f}")

# ---- fresh alpha grid on pooled OOF probabilities (0.00-1.00, step 0.05) ----
grid = [(a, macro_f1(y, blend(oof_text, oof_id, a))) for a in ALPHA_GRID]
chosen_alpha, best_f1 = max(grid, key=lambda t: t[1])
print("alpha grid:", {f"{a:.2f}": round(f, 6) for a, f in grid})
print(f"CHOSEN ALPHA = {chosen_alpha:.2f} | OOF Macro F1 = {best_f1:.6f}")
assert abs(chosen_alpha - 0.40) < 1e-12, "alpha changed - investigate before submitting"

base = blend(oof_text, oof_id, chosen_alpha).argmax(1)
ov_rows = int((oof_override != base).sum())
ov_after = float(f1_score(y, oof_override, average="macro", labels=list(CLASSES),
                          zero_division=0))
print(f"override on OOF: {ov_rows} rows, {best_f1:.6f} -> {ov_after:.6f} "
      f"(delta {ov_after - best_f1:+.6f}) - expected 0 with grouped folds")
"""

CODE_FINAL = r"""
print("=== final model: refit on the FULL training set ===")
word_f, char_f, clf_f = fit_text_model(clean_texts, y, tag="full")
p_text_test = clf_f.predict_proba(build_matrix(word_f, char_f, test_clean))
p_id_test = id_prior(test_ids, ids, y, tag="test prior")   # pool = ALL train ids
p_test = blend(p_text_test, p_id_test, chosen_alpha)
pred_pure = p_test.argmax(axis=1)

# duplicate-text override: lookup built from TRAIN only, conflicting groups skipped
lookup_full, n_conflict = unanimous_lookup(clean_texts, y)
pred_final, n_hits = apply_override(test_clean, pred_pure, lookup_full)
print(f"final alpha={chosen_alpha:.2f} | mean max prob={p_test.max(axis=1).mean():.4f}")
print(f"override: {n_hits}/{len(test_ids)} test rows matched a unanimous training "
      f"text | {int((pred_final != pred_pure).sum())} labels changed | "
      f"conflicting groups skipped={n_conflict}")

# bias sanity check: predicted test share vs train share, flag drift above 10pp
train_share = {int(c): round(100.0 * float((y == c).mean()), 2) for c in CLASSES}
pred_share = {int(c): round(100.0 * float((pred_final == c).mean()), 2) for c in CLASSES}
drift = {int(c): round(pred_share[c] - train_share[c], 2) for c in CLASSES}
flagged = [int(c) for c in CLASSES if abs(drift[c]) > 10.0]
print("train %:", train_share)
print("pred  %:", pred_share)
print("drift pp:", drift, "-> FLAGGED >10pp" if flagged else "-> all within 10pp")
assert not flagged, f"class share drift exceeds 10pp: {flagged}"
"""

CODE_WRITE = r"""
out_dir = Path("submission")            # RELATIVE path: ./submission/
out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
out_path = out_dir / "submission_1.csv"

sub = pd.DataFrame({"id": test_ids, "y_pred": pred_final.astype(int)})[["id", "y_pred"]]


def validate_frame(df, where):
    problems = []
    if list(df.columns) != ["id", "y_pred"]:
        problems.append(f"columns={list(df.columns)}")
    if len(df) != 11955:
        problems.append(f"rows={len(df)} (expected 11955)")
    if not (df["id"].to_numpy() == sample["id"].to_numpy()).all():
        problems.append("id order differs from sample_submission.csv")
    if int(df.isna().to_numpy().sum()) > 0:
        problems.append("nulls present")
    vals = df["y_pred"].to_numpy()
    if not set(map(int, pd.unique(vals))) <= {0, 1, 2}:
        problems.append(f"labels outside 0/1/2: {sorted(pd.unique(vals))}")
    if vals.dtype.kind not in "iu":
        problems.append(f"dtype not integer: {df['y_pred'].dtype}")
    print(f"VALIDATION {where}:", "OK" if not problems else problems)
    assert not problems, problems
    return True


validate_frame(sub, "BEFORE writing")
sub.to_csv(out_path, index=False, encoding="utf-8")

reread = pd.read_csv(out_path, encoding="utf-8")
validate_frame(reread, "AFTER writing (re-read from disk)")
print("wrote:", out_path.as_posix(), "(relative) ->", out_path.resolve())
print("label counts:", {int(c): int((reread["y_pred"] == c).sum()) for c in CLASSES})
print(f"DONE: alpha={chosen_alpha:.2f}, OOF Macro F1={best_f1:.6f}, "
      f"submission rows={len(reread)}")
"""
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Markdown cells that reference the generated cell inventory (kept after main)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


DUP_MARKER_NOTE = "kept-after-main-final"


MD_OVERRIDE_KEPT_FINAL = f"""
### Duplicate-text override rule

After blending, one *conservative* override is applied:

* group **training** rows by cleaned text,
* keep only groups whose label is **unanimous** across all occurrences
  (conflicting groups are skipped entirely -- 53 such groups in this dataset),
* any **test** row whose cleaned text exactly matches one of those keys takes
  that unanimous label; everything else keeps `argmax(blend)`.

**Leak-safety.** For the OOF measurement the lookup is rebuilt from each fold's
*training split only*, so a validation row can never be overridden using its own
label. Because folds are duplicate-grouped, a validation row's exact twin always
shares its fold, so the override affects **0 OOF rows** under this protocol
(Macro F1 0.639579 -> 0.639579, delta +0.000000). It still affects the test set
(272 rows matched, 39 labels changed), where no fold constraint applies.

### Final chosen alpha (Part-1 re-validation)

**alpha = {PART1_ALPHA:.2f}** on the text model (the id prior gets
`1 - alpha = {1 - PART1_ALPHA:.2f}`), confirmed as the argmax of a 0.00-1.00
grid (step 0.05) over grouped OOF probabilities, with

**OOF Macro F1 = {PART1_OOF:.6f}**
(5-fold `StratifiedGroupKFold`, grouped by cleaned+lowercased text).

The alpha grid is recomputed below, so the printed value is a fresh
reproduction rather than a stored number.
"""



def main() -> None:
    parts = [("md", MD_INTRO), ("code", CODE_ENV), ("md", MD_ARCH),
             ("code", CODE_MODEL), ("code", CODE_DATA), ("md", MD_PRIOR),
             ("code", CODE_PRIOR), ("md", MD_CV), ("code", CODE_CV),
             ("md", MD_OVERRIDE_KEPT_FINAL), ("code", CODE_FINAL), ("code", CODE_WRITE)]
    nb_cells = []
    for i, (kind, src) in enumerate(parts, start=1):
        src = src.strip("\n")
        cell = (nbf.v4.new_markdown_cell(src) if kind == "md"
                else nbf.v4.new_code_cell(src))
        cell["id"] = f"cell-{i:02d}"
        nb_cells.append(cell)
    nb = nbf.v4.new_notebook(cells=nb_cells, metadata={
        "kernelspec": {"display_name": "Python 3", "language": "python",
                       "name": "python3"},
        "language_info": {"name": "python", "version": sys.version.split()[0],
                          "mimetype": "text/x-python",
                          "file_extension": ".py"},
        "title": "HerWILL X UAP Datathon 2026 -- reproducible submission #1",
    })
    nb["nbformat"] = 4
    nb["nbformat_minor"] = 5
    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    nbf.write(nb, str(OUT_IPYNB))
    n_code = sum(1 for kind, _ in parts if kind == "code")
    print(f"wrote {OUT_IPYNB} | cells={len(parts)} (markdown={len(parts) - n_code}, "
          f"code={n_code}) | execution_count set to None (fresh on Run All)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

============================================================
FILE: .\src\revalidate_grouped_cv.py
============================================================

"""
src/revalidate_grouped_cv.py -- PART 1: independent re-validation of the
text + ID-locality-prior blend pipeline (HerWILL X UAP Datathon 2026).

Nothing from a previous run's summary or artifact is used as ground truth: every
number below is recomputed from the three CSVs in a fresh interpreter. The only
values carried over from before are
  (a) the recorded SHA-1 checksums of the protected inputs (Step 1 must PASS),
  (b) the previously chosen alpha = 0.40, which is RE-CONFIRMED, not assumed.

Steps -- all must PASS before Part 2 packaging runs:
  1. byte-identical checksums of train.csv / test.csv / sample_submission.csv
  2. full 5-fold StratifiedGroupKFold CV, group = cleaned + lowercased text
     (this removes the duplicate-text train/val leakage disclosed earlier),
     reporting per-fold and pooled OOF Macro F1 for text-only, id-prior-only and
     the blend at alpha = 0.40, plus a fresh alpha grid (0.00-1.00, step 0.05)
  3. id-prior leak-free proof: per-fold val ids vs prior-pool ids disjoint, and
     an independent naive k=20 nearest-id implementation diffed against the
     production one (must be < 1e-10)
  4. duplicate-text override leak-free: each fold's lookup built ONLY from that
     fold's train split; rows overridden and before/after Macro F1 reported
  5. bias check: predicted test class shares vs train class shares (>10pp flag)
  6. PASS/FAIL summary -> data_checks/part1_revalidation_status.json

Run:  python -u src/revalidate_grouped_cv.py
Exit code 0 = every step PASS (Part 2 allowed); 2 = any FAIL (Part 2 forbidden).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, confusion_matrix, f1_score
from sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedGroupKFold

# Production module: freshly imported in this process (no cached run state).
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import experiment_text_id_blend as prod  # noqa: E402

ROOT = prod.ROOT
LOG_PATH = ROOT / "logs" / "revalidation_grouped.log"
prod.LOG_PATH = LOG_PATH                    # redirect the shared logger to our log

FOLD_SCORES_OUT = ROOT / "reports" / "revalidation_grouped_fold_scores.csv"
METRICS_OUT = ROOT / "reports" / "revalidation_grouped_metrics.json"
OOF_OUT = ROOT / "predictions" / "oof" / "oof_revalidation_grouped.csv"
STATUS_OUT = ROOT / "data_checks" / "part1_revalidation_status.json"

# Recorded checksums (from the earlier verified audit) -- Step 1 compares these.
RECORDED = {
    "train.csv": ("66c6b109c8fd187f4c2c474a09159905d25978cd", 5357729),
    "test.csv": ("9caf35ded5a6fe4b2765cdc3340f192d0994d072", 1290735),
    "sample_submission.csv": ("73345c6143ad4e155bac35de1bf8d5d81ed1d039", 105398),
}

SEED = 42
N_SPLITS = 5
ALPHA_GRID = [round(0.05 * i, 2) for i in range(21)]
ALPHA_PREVIOUS = 0.40
K = 20
EPS = 1e-6
BIAS_THRESHOLD_PP = 10.0
PROB_TOL = 1e-6
DIFF_TOL = 1e-10

CLASSES = (0, 1, 2)


def log(msg: str) -> None:
    prod.log(msg)          # writes to LOG_PATH (redirected above) and echoes


def group_key(text) -> str:
    """Grouping key: cleaned text, lowercased and stripped (grouping ONLY)."""
    return prod.clean_text(text).lower().strip()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Step 3 helper: INDEPENDENT naive k=20 nearest-id implementation.
# Deliberately different algorithm from production: full distance matrix + full
# argsort (no insertion-position window, no partition), processed in blocks.
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def independent_knn_id_prior(query_ids, train_ids, train_labels, k: int = K,
                            eps: float = EPS, n_classes: int = 3,
                            block: int = 300) -> np.ndarray:
    q = np.asarray(query_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    t = np.asarray(train_ids, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    y = np.asarray(train_labels, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    order = np.argsort(t, kind="stable")          # ascending id (documented tie rule)
    s_ids, s_y = t[order], y[order]
    kk = int(min(k, len(s_ids)))
    out = np.empty((len(q), n_classes), dtype=float)
    inf = np.iinfo(np.int64).max
    for start in range(0, len(q), block):
        chunk = q[start:start + block]
        d = np.abs(chunk[:, None] - s_ids[None, :])
        d = np.where(chunk[:, None] == s_ids[None, :], inf, d)   # never pick self
        idx = np.argsort(d, axis=1, kind="stable")[:, :kk]       # naive full sort
        ds = np.take_along_axis(d, idx, axis=1).astype(np.float64)
        ys = s_y[idx]
        good = ds < inf
        w = np.where(good, 1.0 / (np.where(good, ds, 0.0) + eps), 0.0)
        blk = np.stack([(w * (ys == c)).sum(axis=1) for c in range(n_classes)], axis=1)
        tot = blk.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        blk = blk / np.where(tot > 0, tot, 1.0)
        blk[tot.ravel() <= 0] = 1.0 / n_classes
        out[start:start + len(chunk)] = blk
    return out


def macro_f1(y_true, proba) -> float:
    return float(f1_score(y_true, np.asarray(proba).argmax(axis=1), average="macro",
                          labels=list(CLASSES), zero_division=0))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# STEP 1 -- protected inputs must be byte-identical to the recorded checksums
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def step1_checksums() -> tuple[bool, dict]:
    import hashlib

    detail, ok = {}, True
    for name, (sha, size) in RECORDED.items():
        raw = (ROOT / name).read_bytes()
        got_sha, got_size = hashlib.sha1(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)
        match = (got_sha == sha and got_size == size)
        ok = ok and match
        detail[name] = {"recorded_sha1": sha, "actual_sha1": got_sha,
                        "recorded_bytes": size, "actual_bytes": got_size,
                        "match": bool(match)}
        log(f"STEP1 {name}: sha1_match={got_sha == sha} size_match={got_size == size} "
            f"({got_sha})")
    log(f"STEP1 overall: {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}")
    return ok, detail


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# STEP 2 + 3 + 4 -- grouped CV, id-prior proof, override proof
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def step2_grouped_cv(train, clean_texts, y, ids, keys) -> dict:
    # Factorise the group keys as an OBJECT index: a plain np.asarray() on this
    # list would allocate a 7 GiB fixed-width unicode array (see main()).
    groups = pd.factorize(pd.Index(keys, dtype="object"), sort=False)[0]
    sizes = pd.Series(groups).value_counts()
    n_dup_groups = int((sizes > 1).sum())
    rows_in_dups = int(sizes[sizes > 1].sum())
    log(f"groups: n={len(sizes)} duplicate_groups={n_dup_groups} "
        f"rows_in_duplicate_groups={rows_in_dups} ({rows_in_dups / len(y):.2%}) "
        f"max_group_size={int(sizes.max())}")

    sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=N_SPLITS, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)
    fold_ids = np.full(len(y), -1, dtype=int)
    for k, (_tr, va) in enumerate(sgkf.split(np.zeros(len(y)), y, groups)):
        fold_ids[va] = k
    if (fold_ids < 0).any():
        raise SystemExit("STEP2 FAIL: some rows received no fold id")
    gf = pd.DataFrame({"g": groups, "f": fold_ids})
    crossing = int((gf.groupby("g")["f"].nunique() > 1).sum())
    log(f"STEP2 group purity: groups spanning >1 fold = {crossing} "
        f"({'PASS' if crossing == 0 else 'FAIL'})")
    if crossing:
        raise SystemExit(f"STEP2 FAIL: {crossing} text groups span multiple folds")

    oof_text = np.zeros((len(y), 3), dtype=float)
    oof_id = np.zeros((len(y), 3), dtype=float)
    oof_override = np.zeros(len(y), dtype=np.int64)
    fold_rows = []
    for k in range(N_SPLITS):
        va = np.flatnonzero(fold_ids == k)
        tr = np.flatnonzero(fold_ids != k)
        row, p_text, p_id, ov_pred = _one_fold(clean_texts, y, ids, k, va, tr)
        oof_text[va] = p_text
        oof_id[va] = p_id
        oof_override[va] = ov_pred
        fold_rows.append(row)
    return {"fold_ids": fold_ids, "fold_rows": fold_rows, "oof_text": oof_text,
            "oof_id": oof_id, "oof_override": oof_override,
            "groups_crossing_folds": crossing, "n_groups": int(len(sizes)),
            "n_duplicate_groups": n_dup_groups, "rows_in_duplicate_groups": rows_in_dups}


def _one_fold(clean_texts, y, ids, k: int, va, tr):
    """Train + evaluate a single grouped-CV fold; returns its metrics row."""
    va_ids, tr_ids = ids[va], ids[tr]

    # --- Step 3, first half: leak assertions -------------------------------
    overlap = int(np.intersect1d(va_ids, tr_ids).size)
    if overlap:
        raise SystemExit(f"STEP3 FAIL: fold {k}: {overlap} validation ids appear in the "
                         f"prior pool")
    log(f"STEP3 fold {k}: val ids ∩ prior pool = {overlap} "
        f"(pool={len(tr_ids)}, val={len(va_ids)}) -> PASS")

    # --- text model (fold-train only); pass OBJECT arrays, never str lists ---
    word, char, clf, meta = prod.fit_text_model(clean_texts[tr], y[tr], tag=f"rv_fold{k}")
    p_text = np.asarray(clf.predict_proba(
        prod.build_text_matrix(word, char, clean_texts[va])), dtype=float)

    # --- id prior: production vs independent re-implementation -------------
    p_id, idiag = prod.id_prior_probs(va_ids, tr_ids, y[tr], k=K, tag=f"rv_fold{k}")
    p_ind = independent_knn_id_prior(va_ids, tr_ids, y[tr], k=K)
    diff = float(np.abs(p_id - p_ind).max())
    log(f"STEP3 fold {k}: production vs independent k-20 prior max_abs_diff="
        f"{diff:.3g} ({'PASS' if diff < DIFF_TOL else 'FAIL'}, tol={DIFF_TOL:g}); "
        f"mean_neighbours={idiag['mean_neighbours_used']}")
    if diff >= DIFF_TOL:
        raise SystemExit(f"STEP3 FAIL: fold {k} prior diff {diff:.3g} >= {DIFF_TOL:g}")

    # --- metrics -----------------------------------------------------------
    p_blend = prod.blend_probs(p_text, p_id, ALPHA_PREVIOUS)
    m_text = prod.metric_block(y[va], p_text)
    m_id = prod.metric_block(y[va], p_id)
    m_bl = prod.metric_block(y[va], p_blend)

    # --- Step 4: override lookup from THIS fold's train split only ---------
    lookup, ostats = prod.unanimous_lookup(clean_texts[tr], y[tr])
    # Direct proof that the lookup is train-only: every key must come from a
    # training row of this fold (validation/test texts are never inserted).
    train_text_set = set(clean_texts[tr].tolist())
    if not set(lookup).issubset(train_text_set):
        raise SystemExit(f"STEP4 FAIL: fold {k} override lookup contains a text that "
                         f"is not in this fold's train split")
    log(f"STEP4 fold {k}: lookup ⊆ fold train texts "
        f"({len(lookup):,} keys, all present in the {len(tr):,} train rows) -> PASS; "
        f"conflicting groups skipped={ostats['n_conflicting_groups_skipped']}")
    ov_pred, ov_hits = prod.apply_override(clean_texts[va], p_blend.argmax(axis=1), lookup)
    m_ov = float(f1_score(y[va], ov_pred, average="macro", labels=list(CLASSES),
                          zero_division=0))
    log(f"STEP4 fold {k}: override lookup built from {len(tr):,} train rows only -> "
        f"{ov_hits} val rows overridden; macro F1 {m_bl['macro_f1']:.6f} -> {m_ov:.6f} "
        f"({m_ov - m_bl['macro_f1']:+.6f})")
    log(f"STEP2 fold {k}: text={m_text['macro_f1']:.6f} id={m_id['macro_f1']:.6f} "
        f"blend@0.40={m_bl['macro_f1']:.6f} | acc text={m_text['accuracy']:.6f} "
        f"blend={m_bl['accuracy']:.6f} | free_ram={prod.ram_free_gb()}GB")

    row = {
        "fold": k, "n_train": int(len(tr)), "n_val": int(len(va)),
        "train_val_id_overlap": overlap,
        "prior_diff_vs_independent": diff,
        "macro_f1_text": round(m_text["macro_f1"], 6),
        "macro_f1_id": round(m_id["macro_f1"], 6),
        "macro_f1_blend_0.40": round(m_bl["macro_f1"], 6),
        "macro_f1_blend_0.40_override": round(m_ov, 6),
        "accuracy_text": round(m_text["accuracy"], 6),
        "accuracy_id": round(m_id["accuracy"], 6),
        "accuracy_blend_0.40": round(m_bl["accuracy"], 6),
        "override_rows": int(ov_hits),
        "override_lookup_texts": int(ostats["n_unanimous_groups"]),
        "override_conflicting_groups_skipped": int(ostats["n_conflicting_groups_skipped"]),
        "text_fit_seconds": meta["fit_seconds"],
    }
    return row, p_text, p_id, ov_pred


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# STEP 2 (pooled) -- OOF metrics + fresh alpha grid
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def pooled_and_grid(y, oof_text, oof_id, oof_override) -> dict:
    eye = np.eye(len(CLASSES))
    pooled = {
        "text_only": prod.metric_block(y, oof_text),
        "id_prior_only": prod.metric_block(y, oof_id),
        "blend_0.40": prod.metric_block(y, prod.blend_probs(oof_text, oof_id,
                                                            ALPHA_PREVIOUS)),
        "blend_0.40_override": prod.metric_block(y, eye[oof_override]),
    }
    grid_rows = []
    for a in ALPHA_GRID:
        m = prod.metric_block(y, prod.blend_probs(oof_text, oof_id, a))
        grid_rows.append({"alpha_text_weight": a, "alpha_id_weight": round(1 - a, 2),
                          **{k: round(v, 6) for k, v in m.items()}})
    grid = pd.DataFrame(grid_rows)
    best_a = float(grid.loc[grid["macro_f1"].idxmax(), "alpha_text_weight"])
    best_f1 = float(grid["macro_f1"].max())
    at_040 = float(grid.loc[grid["alpha_text_weight"] == ALPHA_PREVIOUS,
                            "macro_f1"].iloc[0])
    confirmed = bool(abs(best_a - ALPHA_PREVIOUS) < 1e-12)
    log(f"STEP2 pooled OOF (grouped): text={pooled['text_only']['macro_f1']:.6f} "
        f"id={pooled['id_prior_only']['macro_f1']:.6f} "
        f"blend@0.40={pooled['blend_0.40']['macro_f1']:.6f} "
        f"blend@0.40+override={pooled['blend_0.40_override']['macro_f1']:.6f}")
    log(f"STEP2 alpha grid argmax = {best_a:.2f} (macro F1 {best_f1:.6f}); "
        f"alpha 0.40 -> {at_040:.6f}; 0.40 still argmax = {confirmed}")
    delta = float(pooled["blend_0.40_override"]["macro_f1"]
                  - pooled["blend_0.40"]["macro_f1"])
    base040 = prod.blend_probs(oof_text, oof_id, ALPHA_PREVIOUS).argmax(axis=1)
    changed = int((oof_override != base040).sum())
    log(f"STEP4 pooled OOF override: {changed:,} of {len(y):,} OOF rows overridden; "
        f"macro F1 {pooled['blend_0.40']['macro_f1']:.6f} -> "
        f"{pooled['blend_0.40_override']['macro_f1']:.6f} ({delta:+.6f})")
    return {"pooled": pooled, "grid": grid, "best_alpha": best_a, "best_f1": best_f1,
            "alpha_0.40_macro_f1": at_040, "alpha_040_confirmed_as_argmax": confirmed,
            "override_rows_oof": changed, "override_delta": round(delta, 6)}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# STEP 5 -- bias check on the predicted test-class distribution
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def step5_bias(clean_texts, test_clean, y, train_ids, test_ids, alpha) -> dict:
    log("STEP5: full refit on ALL training rows, then test predictions for the bias check")
    word, char, clf, meta = prod.fit_text_model(clean_texts, y, tag="rv_full")
    p_text = np.asarray(clf.predict_proba(prod.build_text_matrix(word, char, test_clean)),
                        dtype=float)
    p_id, idiag = prod.id_prior_probs(test_ids, train_ids, y, k=K, tag="rv_full_test")
    overlap = int(np.intersect1d(test_ids, train_ids).size)
    if overlap:
        raise SystemExit(f"STEP5 FAIL: {overlap} test ids appear in the id-prior pool")
    log(f"STEP5 id-prior: test ids ∩ prior pool = {overlap} "
        f"(pool={len(train_ids)}, test={len(test_ids)}) -> PASS")

    p = prod.blend_probs(p_text, p_id, alpha)
    pre = p.argmax(axis=1)
    lookup, stats = prod.unanimous_lookup(clean_texts, y)
    post, n_hits = prod.apply_override(test_clean, pre, lookup)

    def shares(v):
        return {int(c): round(100.0 * float((np.asarray(v) == c).mean()), 2)
                for c in CLASSES}

    train_share = shares(y)
    pre_share, post_share = shares(pre), shares(post)
    pre_drift = {int(c): round(pre_share[c] - train_share[c], 2) for c in CLASSES}
    post_drift = {int(c): round(post_share[c] - train_share[c], 2) for c in CLASSES}
    flags_pre = [int(c) for c in CLASSES if abs(pre_drift[c]) > BIAS_THRESHOLD_PP]
    flags_post = [int(c) for c in CLASSES if abs(post_drift[c]) > BIAS_THRESHOLD_PP]

    log(f"STEP5 bias (pre-override):  pred%={pre_share} train%={train_share} "
        f"drift_pp={pre_drift} flagged={flags_pre}")
    log(f"STEP5 bias (post-override): pred%={post_share} train%={train_share} "
        f"drift_pp={post_drift} flagged={flags_post} "
        f"-> {'FAIL' if flags_post else 'PASS'} (threshold {BIAS_THRESHOLD_PP:.0f}pp)")
    log(f"STEP5 test override: {n_hits:,}/{len(test_ids):,} rows matched a unanimous "
        f"training text ({100.0 * n_hits / len(test_ids):.3f}%); "
        f"{int((post != pre).sum()):,} labels changed")
    return {"threshold_pp": BIAS_THRESHOLD_PP, "alpha_used": alpha,
            "train_share_pct": train_share, "test_share_pre_override_pct": pre_share,
            "test_share_post_override_pct": post_share,
            "drift_pre_override_pp": pre_drift, "drift_post_override_pp": post_drift,
            "flagged_pre_override": flags_pre, "flagged_post_override": flags_post,
            "passed": not flags_post,
            "test_rows_overridden": int(n_hits),
            "test_labels_changed": int((post != pre).sum()),
            "lookup_stats": stats, "prior_diag": idiag,
            "mean_max_prob": round(float(p.max(axis=1).mean()), 6),
            "text_full_fit_seconds": meta["fit_seconds"]}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Status gate + main
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def write_status(steps: dict, all_pass: bool, exit_code: int) -> None:
    prod.write_json({"script": "src/revalidate_grouped_cv.py",
                     "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                     "python": sys.version.split()[0], "pid": os.getpid(),
                     "steps": steps, "all_pass": bool(all_pass),
                     "exit_code": int(exit_code)}, STATUS_OUT)
    log(f"wrote {STATUS_OUT.relative_to(ROOT)} all_pass={all_pass} exit_code={exit_code}")


def main() -> int:
    t0 = time.time()
    for d in (prod.REPORTS_DIR, prod.OOF_DIR, prod.DATA_CHECKS_DIR, prod.LOGS_DIR):
        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    log("=" * 78)
    log("PART 1 INDEPENDENT RE-VALIDATION -- fresh process, everything recomputed")
    log(f"python={sys.version.split()[0]} numpy={np.__version__} pid={os.getpid()}")

    # ---------------- STEP 1: checksums (abort on failure) -----------------
    ok1, detail1 = step1_checksums()
    if not ok1:
        write_status({"1_checksums": {"status": "FAIL", "detail": detail1}}, False, 2)
        log("ABORT: protected inputs differ from recorded checksums -> Part 2 FORBIDDEN")
        return 2
    steps = {"1_checksums": {"status": "PASS", "detail": detail1}}

    # ---------------- fresh load -------------------------------------------
    # NOTE: keep texts as a pandas object column -> numpy OBJECT array. Converting
    # a python list of strings with np.asarray would allocate a giant fixed-width
    # <U<len> array (~7 GiB here) and die. Object dtype stores each string alone.
    train, test, sample = prod.load_data()
    y = train["y"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
    ids = train["id"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    test_ids = test["id"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    clean_texts = train["clean"].to_numpy()
    test_clean = test["clean"].to_numpy()
    keys = pd.Index([str(t).lower().strip() for t in clean_texts], dtype="object")

    # ---------------- STEPS 2/3/4: grouped CV ------------------------------
    cv = step2_grouped_cv(train, clean_texts, y, ids, keys)
    pg = pooled_and_grid(y, cv["oof_text"], cv["oof_id"], cv["oof_override"])
    fold_df = pd.DataFrame(cv["fold_rows"])

    step2_ok = (len(cv["fold_rows"]) == N_SPLITS and cv["groups_crossing_folds"] == 0
                and pg["best_f1"] > 0)
    steps["2_grouped_cv"] = {
        "status": "PASS" if step2_ok else "FAIL",
        "strategy": "StratifiedGroupKFold", "n_splits": N_SPLITS, "shuffle": True,
        "random_state": SEED, "group_key": "clean_text(text).lower().strip()",
        "n_groups": cv["n_groups"], "duplicate_groups": cv["n_duplicate_groups"],
        "rows_in_duplicate_groups": cv["rows_in_duplicate_groups"],
        "groups_spanning_folds": cv["groups_crossing_folds"],
        "per_fold": cv["fold_rows"],
        "pooled_macro_f1": {k: round(v["macro_f1"], 6) for k, v in pg["pooled"].items()},
        "alpha_grid": pg["grid"].to_dict(orient="records"),
        "best_alpha": pg["best_alpha"], "best_macro_f1": round(pg["best_f1"], 6),
        "alpha_0.40_macro_f1": round(pg["alpha_0.40_macro_f1"], 6),
        "alpha_040_confirmed_as_argmax": pg["alpha_040_confirmed_as_argmax"],
    }

    step3_ok = (all(int(r["train_val_id_overlap"]) == 0 for r in cv["fold_rows"])
                and all(float(r["prior_diff_vs_independent"]) < DIFF_TOL
                        for r in cv["fold_rows"]))
    steps["3_id_prior_leak_free"] = {
        "status": "PASS" if step3_ok else "FAIL",
        "per_fold_val_id_overlap": [int(r["train_val_id_overlap"])
                                    for r in cv["fold_rows"]],
        "per_fold_max_abs_diff_vs_independent": [
            float(r["prior_diff_vs_independent"]) for r in cv["fold_rows"]],
        "tolerance": DIFF_TOL,
        "independent_implementation": "full distance matrix + full stable argsort "
                                      "(no insertion window, no partition)",
        "k": K, "weights": f"1/(distance+{EPS})",
        "final_test_prior_overlap_checked_in_step5": True,
    }

    steps["4_override_leak_free"] = {
        "status": "PASS",
        "lookup_source": "each fold's train split ONLY (asserted subset of that fold's "
                         "train texts inside _one_fold); test data never used",
        "per_fold_rows_overridden": [int(r["override_rows"]) for r in cv["fold_rows"]],
        "per_fold_conflicting_groups_skipped": [
            int(r["override_conflicting_groups_skipped"]) for r in cv["fold_rows"]],
        "oof_rows_overridden": int(pg["override_rows_oof"]),
        "oof_macro_f1_before": round(pg["pooled"]["blend_0.40"]["macro_f1"], 6),
        "oof_macro_f1_after": round(pg["pooled"]["blend_0.40_override"]["macro_f1"], 6),
        "oof_macro_f1_delta": pg["override_delta"],
    }

    # ---------------- STEP 5: bias check -----------------------------------
    chosen_alpha = pg["best_alpha"]
    bias = step5_bias(clean_texts, test_clean, y, ids, test_ids, chosen_alpha)
    steps["5_bias"] = {"status": "PASS" if bias["passed"] else "FAIL", **bias}

    all_pass = all(s["status"] == "PASS" for s in steps.values())
    return _finish(cv, pg, fold_df, steps, all_pass, bias, chosen_alpha, train, test,
                   sample, y, ids, t0)


def _finish(cv, pg, fold_df, steps, all_pass, bias, chosen_alpha, train, test, sample,
            y, ids, t0) -> int:
    """Write artifacts + status, print the PASS/FAIL summary, return the exit code."""
    prod.atomic_write_csv(fold_df, FOLD_SCORES_OUT)
    log(f"wrote {FOLD_SCORES_OUT.relative_to(ROOT)} rows={len(fold_df)}")

    oof = pd.DataFrame({
        "id": train["id"].to_numpy(), "y_true": y,
        **{c: v for c, v in zip(prod.PROB_COLS,
                                prod.blend_probs(cv["oof_text"], cv["oof_id"],
                                                 chosen_alpha).T)},
        "y_pred": prod.blend_probs(cv["oof_text"], cv["oof_id"],
                                   chosen_alpha).argmax(axis=1),
        "fold": cv["fold_ids"],
        "model_name": f"blend_text_id_a{chosen_alpha:.2f}_groupedcv",
    })[prod.OOF_COLUMNS]
    prod.atomic_write_csv(oof, OOF_OUT)
    log(f"wrote {OOF_OUT.relative_to(ROOT)} rows={len(oof)}")

    metrics = {
        "experiment": "revalidate_grouped_cv",
        "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "python": sys.version.split()[0], "numpy": np.__version__, "pid": os.getpid(),
        "recorded_checksums": steps["1_checksums"]["detail"],
        "validation": {"strategy": "StratifiedGroupKFold", "n_splits": N_SPLITS,
                       "shuffle": True, "random_state": SEED,
                       "group_key": "clean_text(text).lower().strip()"},
        "fold_scores": cv["fold_rows"],
        "pooled_oof_macro_f1": {k: round(v["macro_f1"], 6) for k, v in pg["pooled"].items()},
        "pooled_oof_accuracy": {k: round(v["accuracy"], 6) for k, v in pg["pooled"].items()},
        "pooled_oof_per_class": {k: {f"f1_class{c}": round(v[f"f1_class{c}"], 6)
                                     for c in CLASSES}
                                 for k, v in pg["pooled"].items()},
        "alpha_grid": pg["grid"].to_dict(orient="records"),
        "chosen_alpha": chosen_alpha, "chosen_macro_f1": round(pg["best_f1"], 6),
        "alpha_0.40_macro_f1": round(pg["alpha_0.40_macro_f1"], 6),
        "alpha_040_confirmed_as_argmax": pg["alpha_040_confirmed_as_argmax"],
        "bias_check": bias,
        "steps": steps, "all_pass": bool(all_pass),
        "runtime_seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 2),
        "outputs": {"fold_scores": str(FOLD_SCORES_OUT.relative_to(ROOT)),
                    "oof": str(OOF_OUT.relative_to(ROOT)),
                    "metrics": str(METRICS_OUT.relative_to(ROOT)),
                    "status": str(STATUS_OUT.relative_to(ROOT))},
    }
    prod.write_json(metrics, METRICS_OUT)
    log(f"wrote {METRICS_OUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    write_status(steps, all_pass, 0 if all_pass else 2)

    # ---------------- PASS/FAIL summary -------------------------------------
    lines = ["", "=" * 76,
             "PART 1 -- FINAL PASS/FAIL SUMMARY (all values recomputed this run)",
             "=" * 76]
    for name, s in steps.items():
        lines.append(f"  {name}: {s['status']}")
    lines += [
        f"  1 checksums   : " + "; ".join(
            f"{k}={'OK' if v['match'] else 'DIFF'}" for k, v in
            steps["1_checksums"]["detail"].items()),
        f"  2 grouped CV  : folds=5 groups={steps['2_grouped_cv']['n_groups']} "
        f"dup_groups={steps['2_grouped_cv']['duplicate_groups']} "
        f"crossing={steps['2_grouped_cv']['groups_spanning_folds']} | pooled OOF "
        f"text={pg['pooled']['text_only']['macro_f1']:.6f} "
        f"id={pg['pooled']['id_prior_only']['macro_f1']:.6f} "
        f"blend@0.40={pg['pooled']['blend_0.40']['macro_f1']:.6f} "
        f"blend+override={pg['pooled']['blend_0.40_override']['macro_f1']:.6f}",
        f"      alpha grid argmax={pg['best_alpha']:.2f} "
        f"(macro F1 {pg['best_f1']:.6f}); 0.40 confirmed as argmax="
        f"{pg['alpha_040_confirmed_as_argmax']}",
        f"  3 id-prior    : val∩pool={steps['3_id_prior_leak_free']['per_fold_val_id_overlap']} "
        f"max_diffs={steps['3_id_prior_leak_free']['per_fold_max_abs_diff_vs_independent']} "
        f"(tol={DIFF_TOL:g})",
        f"  4 override    : OOF rows overridden="
        f"{steps['4_override_leak_free']['oof_rows_overridden']:,} "
        f"macro F1 {steps['4_override_leak_free']['oof_macro_f1_before']:.6f} -> "
        f"{steps['4_override_leak_free']['oof_macro_f1_after']:.6f} "
        f"(delta {steps['4_override_leak_free']['oof_macro_f1_delta']:+.6f}); "
        f"lookup=train-split-only",
        f"  5 bias        : train%={bias['train_share_pct']} "
        f"test%={bias['test_share_post_override_pct']} "
        f"drift_pp={bias['drift_post_override_pp']} "
        f"flagged={bias['flagged_post_override']} (>{BIAS_THRESHOLD_PP:.0f}pp)",
        f"  RESULT: {'ALL PASS -> Part 2 may proceed' if all_pass else 'FAILURE -> Part 2 FORBIDDEN'}",
        f"  chosen_alpha={chosen_alpha:.2f} | grouped-OOF macro F1="
        f"{pg['best_f1']:.6f} | runtime={metrics['runtime_seconds']}s",
        "=" * 76,
    ]
    for line in lines:
        log(line)
    return 0 if all_pass else 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        sys.exit(main())
    except SystemExit:
        raise
    except Exception:
        import traceback

        log("PART 1 FAILED -- traceback follows")
        log(traceback.format_exc())
        write_status({"harness": {"status": "FAIL",
                                  "error": traceback.format_exc()[-800:]}}, False, 2)
        raise








============================================================
FILE: .\src\utils.py
